{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787409758505,"sessionID":"ses_fd613a9a0ffeanefk4okv1NNUn","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"read","callID":"call_01a029ec72db71b2ab4c2184103ce0b2","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/src/pvf/pvf_app_runner.py"},"output":"<path>/workspaces/powerchoice/src/pvf/pvf_app_runner.py</path>\n<type>file</type>\n<content>\n1: \"\"\"pvf_app_runner — the standard, executable application runner for pvf-based applications.\n2: \n3: Startup orchestration:\n4:   1. load pvf_app_startup.yaml (bindings, feature switches, share vocabularies)\n5:   2. ingest the configured env file (usually .env) with python-dotenv\n6:   3. inject startup-owned values into pvf_settings (read-only thereafter)\n7:   4. establish the database connection FIRST; on failure, clear diagnostics to stderr\n8:      and exit with an error code\n9:   5. register pvf model metadata (feature-gated)\n10:   6. build the four FastAPI applications (noauth / session / share / external) and the\n11:      root app; register pvf-owned routers\n12:   7. invoke the application shell entry point with a PvfInvocation; it registers hooks\n13:      and routers and returns None (success), an error message, or raises\n14:   8. complete setup: include application routers, merge OpenAPI tag metadata, run the\n15:      dependency-injection integrity check, install handlers, mount apps and static files\n16: \n17: Run with:  fastapi dev src/pvf/pvf_app_runner.py   (or fastapi run ... for production)\n18: The module-level `app` is the composed ASGI root application.\n19: \"\"\"\n20: import importlib\n21: import json\n22: import os\n23: import re\n24: import sys\n25: from pathlib import Path\n26: from traceback import format_exc\n27: \n28: from fastapi import FastAPI\n29: from fastapi.exceptions import RequestValidationError\n30: from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware\n31: from fastapi.openapi.docs import get_swagger_ui_html\n32: from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse\n33: from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles\n34: from sqlalchemy import text\n35: \n36:     \n37: from .bindings.pvf_startup_config import (\n38:     PvfStartupConfig,\n39:     PvfStartupConfigError,\n40:     apply_startup_config,\n41:     load_startup_config,\n42: )\n43: \n44: _WORKSPACE_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]  # pvf -> src -> workspace root\n45: _STATIC_DIR = str(_WORKSPACE_ROOT / \"static\")\n46: _STATIC_CLIENT_DIR = str(_WORKSPACE_ROOT / \"static_client\")\n47: \n48: # Mount prefixes for the four FastAPI applications\n49: MOUNT_PREFIXES = {\"noauth\": \"/auth-ws\", \"session\": \"/ws\", \"share\": \"/ext-ws\", \"external\": \"/api\"}\n50: \n51: # Non-app paths (reserved prefixes) always return JSON 404s rather than the SPA page\n52: RESERVED_PREFIXES = [\"/ws\", \"/ext-ws\", \"/api\", \"/auth\", \"/hook-stripe-events\", \"/api/webhooks\"]\n53: \n54: EXIT_BAD_STARTUP_CONFIG = 2\n55: EXIT_DATABASE_FAILED = 3\n56: EXIT_APP_STARTUP_FAILED = 4\n57: \n58: # The composed applications, populated by build_app(); consumed by tooling\n59: # (OpenAPI snapshot extraction, diagnostics).\n60: composed_apps: dict[str, FastAPI] = {}\n61: \n62: _PVF_TAGS = {\n63:     \"noauth\": [\n64:         {\"name\": \"auth\", \"description\": \"Services to support authentication\"},\n65:         {\"name\": \"self-reg\", \"description\": \"Self Registration Services for Initial Signup\"},\n66:     ],\n67:     \"session\": [\n68:         {\"name\": \"admin\", \"description\": \"Services to customer/user administration and customer/user information\"},\n69:         {\"name\": \"context\", \"description\": \"Services to access the user/customer context and related user information\"},\n70:         {\"name\": \"devops\", \"description\": \"Services to access and post log events for diagnostic purposes\"},\n71:     ],\n72:     \"share\": [],\n73:     \"external\": [\n74:         {\"name\": \"access_check\", \"description\": \"Signed API connectivity checks\"},\n75:     ],\n76: }\n77: \n78: \n79: def _fatal(message: str, exit_code: int) -> None:\n80:     print(f\"FATAL: {message}\", file=sys.stderr)\n81:     raise SystemExit(exit_code)\n82: \n83: \n84: def _load_env_file(startup_config: PvfStartupConfig) -> None:\n85:     \"\"\"Ingest the configured env file (usually .env). Process environment wins.\"\"\"\n86:     from dotenv import load_dotenv\n87: \n88:     env_path = Path(startup_config.env_file)\n89:     if not env_path.is_absolute():\n90:         env_path = _WORKSPACE_ROOT / env_path\n91:     if env_path.is_file():\n92:         load_dotenv(env_path, override=False)\n93: \n94: \n95: def _probe_database_or_exit(db, dsn: str) -> None:\n96:     try:\n97:         with db.get_engine().connect() as connection:\n98:             connection.execute(text(\"SELECT 1\"))\n99:     except Exception as ex:\n100:         masked_dsn = re.sub(r\"://([^:@/]+):([^@/]+)@\", r\"://\\1:****@\", dsn)\n101:         _fatal(\n102:             \"could not establish a connection to the database.\\n\"\n103:             f\"  DSN: {masked_dsn}\\n\"\n104:             f\"  error: {type(ex).__name__}: {ex}\",\n105:             EXIT_DATABASE_FAILED,\n106:         )\n107: \n108: \n109: def _merge_openapi_specs(title: str, specs: list[tuple[str, FastAPI]]) -> dict:\n110:     \"\"\"Merge (mount_prefix, app) OpenAPI specs into one schema; paths are prefixed.\n111: \n112:     Component name collisions are tolerated only when the schemas are identical\n113:     (shared pvf models); conflicting definitions are a build error.\n114:     \"\"\"\n115:     merged: dict | None = None\n116:     for prefix, sub_app in specs:\n117:         spec = sub_app.openapi()\n118:         if merged is None:\n119:             merged = {\"openapi\": spec[\"openapi\"], \"info\": {\"title\": title, \"version\": spec[\"info\"].get(\"version\", \"\")},\n120:                       \"paths\": {}, \"components\": {\"schemas\": {}}, \"tags\": []}\n121:         for path, item in spec.get(\"paths\", {}).items():\n122:             merged[\"paths\"][prefix + path] = item\n123:         for name, schema in spec.get(\"components\", {}).get(\"schemas\", {}).items():\n124:             existing = merged[\"components\"][\"schemas\"].get(name)\n125:             if existing is not None and existing != schema:\n126:                 raise RuntimeError(f\"Conflicting OpenAPI schema definitions for '{name}' across composed apps\")\n127:             merged[\"components\"][\"schemas\"][name] = schema\n128:         seen_tags = {t[\"name\"] for t in merged[\"tags\"]}\n129:         for tag in spec.get(\"tags\", []):\n130:             if tag[\"name\"] not in seen_tags:\n131:                 merged[\"tags\"].append(tag)\n132:                 seen_tags.add(tag[\"name\"])\n133:     return executable application runner for pvf-based applications.\n\nStartup orchestration:\n  1. load pvf_app_startup.yaml (bindings, feature switches, share vocabularies)\n  2. ingest the configured env file (usually .env) with python-dotenv\n  3. inject startup-owned values into pvf_settings (read-only thereafter)\n  4. establish the database connection FIRST; on failure, clear diagnostics to stderr\n     and exit with an error code\n  5. register pvf model metadata (feature-gated)\n  6. build the four FastAPI applications (noauth / session / share / external) and the\n     root app; register pvf-owned routers\n  7. invoke the application shell entry point with a PvfInvocation; it registers hooks\n     and routers and returns None (success), an error message, or raises\n  8. complete setup: include application routers, merge OpenAPI tag metadata, run the\n     dependency-injection integrity check, install handlers, mount apps and static files\n\nRun with:  fastapi dev src/pvf/pvf_app_runner.py   (or fastapi run ... for production)\nThe module-level `app` is the composed ASGI root application.\n\"\"\"\nimport importlib\nimport json\nimport os\nimport re\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom traceback import format_exc\n\nfrom fastapi import FastAPI\nfrom fastapi.exceptions import RequestValidationError\nfrom fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware\nfrom fastapi.openapi.docs import get_swagger_ui_html\nfrom fastapi.responses import JSONResponse\nfrom fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles\nfrom sqlalchemy import text\n\n    \nfrom .bindings.pvf_startup_config import (\n    PvfStartupConfig,\n    PvfStartupConfigError,\n    apply_startup_config,\n    load_startup_config,\n)\n\n_WORKSPACE_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]  # pvf -> src -> workspace root\n_STATIC_DIR = str(_WORKSPACE_ROOT / \"static\")\n_STATIC_CLIENT_DIR = str(_WORKSPACE_ROOT / \"static_client\")\n\n# Mount prefixes for the four FastAPI applications\nMOUNT_PREFIXES = {\"noauth\": \"/auth-ws\", \"session\": \"/ws\", \"share\": \"/ext-ws\", \"external\": \"/api\"}\n\n# Non-app paths (reserved prefixes) always return JSON 404s rather than the SPA page\nRESERVED_PREFIXES = [\"/ws\", \"/ext-ws\", \"/api\", \"/auth\", \"/hook-stripe-events\", \"/api/webhooks\"]\n\nEXIT_BAD_STARTUP_CONFIG = 2\nEXIT_DATABASE_FAILED = 3\nEXIT_APP_STARTUP_FAILED = 4\n\n# The composed applications, populated by build_app(); consumed by tooling\n# (OpenAPI snapshot extraction, diagnostics).\ncomposed_apps: dict[str, FastAPI] = {}\n\n_PVF_TAGS = {\n    \"noauth\": [\n        {\"name\": \"auth\", \"description\": \"Services to support authentication\"},\n        {\"name\": \"self-reg\", \"description\": \"Self Registration Services for Initial Signup\"},\n    ],\n    \"session\": [\n        {\"name\": \"admin\", \"description\": \"Services to customer/user administration and customer/user information\"},\n        {\"name\": \"context\", \"description\": \"Services to access the user/customer context and related user information\"},\n        {\"name\": \"devops\", \"description\": \"Services to access and post log events for diagnostic purposes\"},\n    ],\n    \"share\": [],\n    \"external\": [\n        {\"name\": \"access_check\", \"description\": \"Signed API connectivity checks\"},\n    ],\n}\n\n\ndef _fatal(message: str, exit_code: int) -> None:\n    print(f\"FATAL: {message}\", file=sys.stderr)\n    raise SystemExit(exit_code)\n\n\ndef _load_env_file(startup_config: PvfStartupConfig) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Ingest the configured env file (usually .env). Process environment wins.\"\"\"\n    from dotenv import load_dotenv\n\n    env_path = Path(startup_config.env_file)\n    if not env_path.is_absolute():\n        env_path = _WORKSPACE_ROOT / env_path\n    if env_path.is_file():\n        load_dotenv(env_path, override=False)\n\n\ndef _probe_database_or_exit(db, dsn: str) -> None:\n    try:\n        with db.get_engine().connect() as connection:\n            connection.execute(text(\"SELECT 1\"))\n    except Exception as ex:\n        masked_dsn = re.sub(r\"://([^:@/]+):([^@/]+)@\", r\"://\\1:****@\", dsn)\n        _fatal(\n            \"could not establish a connection to the database.\\n\"\n            f\"  DSN: {masked_dsn}\\n\"\n            f\"  error: {type(ex).__name__}: {ex}\",\n            EXIT_DATABASE_FAILED,\n        )\n\n\ndef _merge_openapi_specs(title: str, specs: list[tuple[str, FastAPI]]) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Merge (mount_prefix, app) OpenAPI specs into one schema; paths are prefixed.\n\n    Component name collisions are tolerated only when the schemas are identical\n    (shared pvf models); conflicting definitions are a build error.\n    \"\"\"\n    merged: dict | None = None\n    for prefix, sub_app in specs:\n        spec = sub_app.openapi()\n        if merged is None:\n            merged = {\"openapi\": spec[\"openapi\"], \"info\": {\"title\": title, \"version\": spec[\"info\"].get(\"version\", \"\")},\n                      \"paths\": {}, \"components\": {\"schemas\": {}}, \"tags\": []}\n        for path, item in spec.get(\"paths\", {}).items():\n            merged[\"paths\"][prefix + path] = item\n        for name, schema in spec.get(\"components\", {}).get(\"schemas\", {}).items():\n            existing = merged[\"components\"][\"schemas\"].get(name)\n            if existing is not None and existing != schema:\n                raise RuntimeError(f\"Conflicting OpenAPI schema definitions for '{name}' across composed apps\")\n            merged[\"components\"][\"schemas\"][name] = schema\n        seen_tags = {t[\"name\"] for t in merged[\"tags\"]}\n        for tag in spec.get(\"tags\", []):\n            if tag[\"name\"] not in seen_tags:\n                merged[\"tags\"].append(tag)\n                seen_tags.add(tag[\"name\"])\n    return merged or {}\n\n\n_sessions_openapi_cache: dict = {}\n\n\ndef build_sessions_openapi() -> dict:\n    \"\"\"The aggregate session-facing contract: root + noauth + session + share apps.\"\"\"\n    if not _sessions_openapi_cache:\n        specs = [(\"\", composed_apps[\"root\"])]\n        for target in (\"noauth\", \"session\", \"share\"):\n            specs.append((MOUNT_PREFIXES[target], composed_apps[target]))\n        _sessions_openapi_cache.update(_merge_openapi_specs(\"Session UI API\", specs))\n    return _sessions_openapi_cache\n\n\ndef build_external_openapi() -> dict:\n    return composed_apps[\"external\"].openapi()\n\n\ndef build_app(config_path: str | None = None) -> FastAPI:\n    global composed_apps\n\n    # 1. startup YAML\n    try:\n        startup_config = load_startup_config(config_path)\n    except PvfStartupConfigError as ex:\n        _fatal(str(ex), EXIT_BAD_STARTUP_CONFIG)\n\n    # 2. env file (python-dotenv; process environment overrides file values)\n    _load_env_file(startup_config)\n\n    # 3. settings + read-only startup value injection\n    from .config.pvf_config_settings import pvf_settings\n\n    apply_startup_config(startup_config, pvf_settings)\n    pvf_settings.process_name = os.path.basename(__file__)\n\n    if not pvf_settings.UNIQUE_CONFIGURATION_CHECK:\n        _fatal(\n            \"This system appears to be running on an example or missing configuration file; \"\n            \"check the .env contents (UNIQUE_CONFIGURATION_CHECK must be 1).\",\n            EXIT_BAD_STARTUP_CONFIG,\n        )\n\n    # 4. database connection first\n    from .db.connect import DatabaseConnection\n\n    db = DatabaseConnection()\n    _probe_database_or_exit(db, pvf_settings.DB_PATH_OR_CONNECTION_STRING)\n\n    # 4b. application schema extensions must be declared before any model import:\n    # import the shell module (declarations only; no models at module level by\n    # contract) and let it register added columns on pvf tables\n    try:\n        shell_module = importlib.import_module(startup_config.application.shell_module)\n    except Exception as ex:\n        _fatal(\n            f\"could not import application shell module '{startup_config.application.shell_module}': {ex}\",\n            EXIT_APP_STARTUP_FAILED,\n        )\n    from .db.model_factory import get_schema_extension_registry\n\n    register_schema_extensions = getattr(shell_module, \"register_schema_extensions\", None)\n    if callable(register_schema_extensions):\n        register_schema_extensions(get_schema_extension_registry())\n\n    # 5. pvf model metadata (feature-gated)\n    from .db.models import pvf_bootstrap\n\n    pvf_bootstrap.import_core_models()\n    pvf_bootstrap.import_feature_models(\n        activate_stripe_integration=pvf_settings.ACTIVATE_STRIPE_INTEGRATION,\n        activate_branding_image_store=pvf_settings.ACTIVATE_BRANDING_IMAGE_STORE,\n        activate_share_links=pvf_settings.ACTIVATE_SHARE_LINKS,\n        activate_external_api=pvf_settings.ACTIVATE_EXTERNAL_API,\n        activate_customer_communication=pvf_settings.ACTIVATE_CUSTOMER_COMMUNICATION,\n    )\n\n    # 6. build the FastAPI applications\n    from .bindings.pvf_invocation import PvfAppTarget, PvfInvocation, set_current_invocation\n    from .utils.fastapi_app_handlers import (\n        handle_500_internal_error,\n        handle_422_unprocessable_entity,\n        make_spa_fallback_404_handler,\n    )\n\n    # per-app 404 defaults to JSON; the root app gets the SPA fallback below\n    sub_app_exceptions: dict = {}\n    if pvf_settings.PYTEST_ACTIVE is False:\n        # don't handle 500 errors while testing, so complete error message is visible in pytest results\n        sub_app_exceptions[500] = handle_500_internal_error\n\n    def _make_sub_app(title: str, target: str) -> FastAPI:\n        sub = FastAPI(title=title, openapi_tags=list(_PVF_TAGS[target]), exception_handlers=dict(sub_app_exceptions))\n\n        @sub.exception_handler(RequestValidationError)\n        async def   return raw\n    if isinstance(raw, str):\n        try:\n            data = json.loads(raw)\n            return data if isinstance(data, dict) else {}\n        except Exception:\n            return {}\n    return {}\n\n\ndef _client_settings_defaults():\n    inv = invocation_context\n    if inv is not None and inv.app_client_settings is not None:\n        return copy.deepcopy(inv.app_client_settings)\n    settings_type = inv.client_session_context_settings_type if inv is not None else None\n    if settings_type is not None:\n        return settings_type()\n    return PvfClientAuthSettings()\n\n\ndef _user_communication_available() -> bool:\n    return bool(getattr(runtime_settings(), \"ACTIVATE_CUSTOMER_COMMUNICATION\", False))\n\n\ndef _ai_yaml_activated() -> bool:\n    return bool(getattr(runtime_settings(), \"ACTIVATE_AI_AGENTS\", False))\n\n\ndef _ai_features_available() -> bool:\n    return _ai_yaml_activated()\n\n\ndef _customer_llm_configured(customer) -> bool:\n    if customer is None:\n        return False\n    try:\n        return bool(customer.customer_llm_configured())\n    except Exception:\n        provider = str(getattr(customer, \"ai_provider\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n        key = decrypt_secret(getattr(customer, \"ai_api_key\", None) or \"\").strip()\n        return bool(provider) and provider_is_supported(provider) and bool(key)\n\n\ndef _ai_providers_configured(customer=None) -> bool:\n    if not _ai_yaml_activated():\n        return False\n    return system_llm_configured() or _customer_llm_configured(customer)\n\n\ndef _merged_client_settings(*blobs):\n    merged = _client_settings_defaults()\n    for raw in blobs:\n        data = _parse_settings_blob(raw)\n        for k, v in data.items():\n            if hasattr(merged, k):\n                setattr(merged, k, v)\n    if hasattr(merged, \"enable_user_communication\"):\n        merged.enable_user_communication = _user_communication_available()\n    if hasattr(merged, \"ai_features_enabled\"):\n        merged.ai_features_enabled = _ai_features_available()\n    if hasattr(merged, \"ai_providers_configured\"):\n Field(default=None, description=\"Prefix for stripe callback URLs associated with the customer in sandbox mode (forces livemode False)\")\n    user_record: User = Field(default=None, for client settings; from global config_client, with potential customer or user overrides\")\n\n    @model_serializer(mode=\"wrap\")\n    def _serialize_masked_context(self, serializer):\n        data = serializer(self)\n        masked_fields = list(runtime_settings().CONTEXT_MASKED_FIELDS or [])\n        for extra in (\"ai_api_key\",):\n            if extra not in masked_fields:\n                masked_fields.append(extra)\n        if not masked_fields:\n            return data\n        for record_key in (\"user_record\", \"customer_record\"):\n            record = data.get(record_key)\n data\n\n@noauth_router.post(\"/login-and-get-context\",\n             summary=\"Create access and refresh tokens for user session and return session settings/context\",\n             tags=['auth', id=usr_context.sess_user.customer_id)\n        if cur_customer is None:\n            log_event(f'Customer id {usr_context.sess_user.customer_id} not found for current user {usr_context.sess_user.id}',       check_trial_expiration_days = (check_expiration_date - datetime.now(timezone.utc)).days\n        if check_trial_expiration_days >= 0:\n            trial_expiration_days = trial_expiration_days=trial_expiration_days,\n                                            is_customer_admin=usr_context.sess_user.customer_admin,\n                                            user_record=usr_context.sess_user,\n                                            customer_record=cur_customer,\n                                            )\n    context_view.customer_name = usr_context.sess_user.client_settings,\n    )\n    if hasattr(context_view.settings, \"ai_providers_configured\"):\n        context_view.settings.ai_providers_configured = _ai_providers_configured(cur_customer)\n    apply_2fa_client_settings(context_view.settings, usr_context.sess_user, cur_customer)\n\n    account_users_check = User.get_account_users(session=session, usr_context=usr_context)\n    if account_users_check.failure_reason != '':\n        context_view.failure_reason = account_users_check.failure_reason\n        context_view.log_id = account_users_check.log_id\n        return context_view\n\n    context_view.team_info = []\n    for account_user_row in account_users_check.user_info_list:\n        context_view.team_info.append(TeamMember(id=account_user_row.id,\n                                                 name=account_user_row.name,\n                                                 email=account_user_row.email,\n                                                 customer_admin=account_user_row.customer_admin,\n                                                 created_date=account_user_row.create_date))\n    return context_view\n\n\n@router.post(\n    \"/core/update-customer-vote-settings\",\n    summary=\"Admin: update customer-level compare/results CustomerVoteSettingsResult:\n    if not usr_context.sess_user.customer_admin:\n        log_id = log_event(\n            \"Non-admin attempted to update customer vote settings\",\n            usr_context=usr_context,\n            severity=3,\n        )\n        return CustomerVoteSettingsResult(failure_reason=\"Customer admin access required\", log_id=log_id)\n\n    customer = usr_context.sess_customer\n    if customer is None:\n        customer = Customer.get_customer_by_id_system(\n            session=session, id=usr_context.sess_user.customer_id, clear_lock=False\n        )\n    if customer is None:\n        return CustomerVoteSettingsResult(failure_reason=\"Customer not found\")\n\n    data = _parse_settings_blob(customer.client_settings)\n    # Strip retired SoftMax / triple-frame knobs\n    if form.coherence_method is not None:\n        m = str(form.coherence_method).strip().lower()\n        data[\"coherence_method\"] = m if m in (\"spearman\", \"kendall\") else \"spearman\"\n    customer.client_settings = json.dumps(data) if data else None\n    customer.update_customer_system(session=session, clear_lock=False)\n    merged = _merged_client_settings(customer.client_settings, usr_context.sess_user.client_settings)\n    apply_2fa_client_settings(merged, usr_context.sess_user, customer)\n    return CustomerVoteSettingsResult(settings=merged)\n\n\nclass CustomerAiSettingsForm(BaseModel):\n    ai_provider: str | None = Field(default=None, description=\"opencode_go, opencode_zen, or openrouter\")\n    ai_model: str | None = Field(default=None, description=\"Default model identifier for this customer\")\n    ai_api_key: str | None = Field(default=None, description=\"Authorization key; send stored encoded value to leave unchanged, blank to clear\")\n\n\nclass CustomerAiSettingsResult(WsResultPackage):\n    ai_provider: str | None = None\n    ai_model: str | None = None\n    ai_api_key: str | None = None\n    key_configured: bool = False\n    system_provider_configured: bool = False\n    providers: list[str] = Field(default_factory=lambda: list(SUPPORTED_LLM_PROVIDERS))\n\n\nclass | None:\n    if not usr_context.sess_user.customer_admin:\n        log_event(\n            \"Non-admin attempted to update customer AI settings\",\n            usr_context=usr_context,\n            severity=3,\n        )\n        return \"Customer admin access required\"\n    return None\n\n\ndef _load_session_customer(session: Session, usr_context) -> Customer | None:\n    customer = usr_context.sess_customer\n    if customer is None:\n        customer = Customer.get_customer_by_id_system(\n            session=session, id=usr_context.sess_user.customer_id, clear_lock=False\n        )\n    return customer\n\n\ndef _ai_settings_view(customer: Customer) -> CustomerAiSettingsResult:\n    stored_key = customer.ai_api_key or \"\"\n    return CustomerAiSettingsResult(\n        ai_provider=customer.ai_provider or None,\n        ai_model=customer.ai_model or None,\n        ai_api_key=stored_key,\n        key_configured=bool(customer.decrypted_ai_api_key().strip()),\n        system_provider_configured=system_llm_configured(),\n        providers=list(SUPPORTED_LLM_PROVIDERS),\n    )\n\n\ndef _resolve_submitted_key(customer: Customer, submitted: str | None) -> str:\n    value = \"\" if submitted is None else str(submitted)\n    stored = customer.ai_api_key or \"\"\n    if value == stored or is_stored_secret(value):\n        return customer.decrypted_ai_api_key()\n    return value\n\n\n@router.get(\n    \"/core/customer-ai-settings\",\n    summary=\"Admin: read customer LLM provider, model, and encoded key\",\n    tags=[\"context\"],\n)\nasync def get_customer_ai_settings(\n    session: SessionDep,\n    usr_context: UserAccessDep,\n) -> CustomerAiSettingsResult:\n    if not _ai_yaml_activated():\n        return _require_customer_admin(usr_context)\n    if denied:\n        return CustomerAiSettingsResult(failure_reason=denied)\n    customer = _load_session_customer(session, usr_context)\n    if customer is normalize_llm_provider(provider)\n        if not provider_is_supported(provider):\n            return CustomerAiSettingsResult(failure_reason=\"Unsupported LLM provider\")\n        customer.ai_provider = provider\n    else:\n        customer.ai_provider = None\n    customer.ai_model = (str(form.ai_model or \"\").strip() or None)\n    customer.apply_ai_api_key_update(form.ai_api_key)\n    customer.update_customer_system(session=session, clear_lock=False)\n    return _ai_settings_view(customer)\n\n\n@router.post(\n    \"/core/test-customer-ai-connection\",\n    summary=\"Admin: test LLM provider credentials and list available models\",\n    tags=[\"context\"],\n)\nasync def test_customer_ai_connection(\n TestCustomerAiConnectionResult(failure_reason=\"AI features are not enabled\")\n    denied = _require_customer_admin(usr_context)\n    if denied:\n        return TestCustomerAiConnectionResult(failure_reason=denied)\n    customer = _load_session_customer(session, usr_context)\n    if customer is None:\n        return TestCustomerAiConnectionResult(failure_reason=\"Customer not found\")\n    provider = normalize_llm_provider(form.ai_provider)\n    if not provider_is_supported(provider):\n        return TestCustomerAiConnectionResult(failure_reason=\"Unsupported LLM provider\")\n    key = _resolve_submitted_key(customer, form.ai_api_key)\n    if not key:\n        return TestCustomerAiConnectionResult(failure_reason=NO_PROVIDERS_CONFIGURED)\n    verified = verify_llm_provider(provider, key, form.ai_model)\n    return TestCustomerAiConnectionResult(\n        ok=bool(verified.get(\"ok\")),\n        models=list(verified.get(\"models\") or []),\n        provider=verified.get(\"provider\") or provider,\n        failure_reason=\"\" if verified.get(\"ok\") else {\n        return result\n      }\n      await refreshContext()\n      return result\n    }\n    finally {\n      loading.value = false\n    }\n  }\n\n  async function logout() {\n    try {\n      await apiFetch<{ message?: string }>('/auth-ws/logout', { method: 'GET' })\n    }\n    catch {\n      // still clear local session\n    }\n    context.value = null\n    initialized.value = true\n await refreshContext()\n    return isAuthenticated.value\n  }\n\n  async function signup(payload: CustomerSelfRegPayload) {\n    return apiFetch<CustomerUserCreateResult>('/auth-ws/initial-signup', {"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787409764207,"sessionID":"ses_fd613a9a0ffeanefk4okv1NNUn","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"read","callID":"call_01a029ec88e671e1873336f76412d22a","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/src/pvf/bindings/pvf_invocation.py"},"output":"<path>/workspaces/powerchoice/src/pvf/bindings/pvf_invocation.py</path>\n<type>file</type>\n<content>\n1: \"\"\"PvfInvocation — the binding contract between pvf_app_runner and the application shell.\n2: \n3: The runner builds this object and passes it to the application entry point named in\n4: pvf_app_startup.yaml. The application is expected to:\n5:   1. create its own settings object (typically subclassing PvfGlobalSettings) and call\n6:      pvf.config.pvf_startup_config.merge_pvf_settings_into() to receive startup-owned values\n7:   2. import its model modules (registering table metadata)\n8:   3. register any hooks connecting framework internals to application logic\n9:   4. add its routers via add_router() targeted at the appropriate FastAPI app\n10:   5. optionally extend openapi_tags and set application_version\n11: The entry point returns None on success, or an error message string on failure; it may\n12: also raise descriptive exceptions to signal startup failure.\n13: \"\"\"\n14: from enum import StrEnum\n15: from typing import Any, Callable\n16: \n17: from fastapi import APIRouter, FastAPI\n18: from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field\n19: \n20: from ..config.pvf_config_settings import PvfGlobalSettings, pvf_settings\n21: from ..utils.pvf_base_internal_resources import PvfClientAuthSettings\n22: \n23: class PvfAppTarget(StrEnum):\n24:     \"\"\"The four FastAPI applications composed by pvf_app_runner.\"\"\"\n25:     noauth = \"noauth\"        # no authorization expected: login, password reset, self registration\n26:     session = \"session\"      # logged-in session (JWT cookie) endpoints\n27:     share = \"share\"          # share link access (cookie / magic-key gate, no session JWT)\n28:     external = \"external\"    # external API (signed headers)\n29: \n30: \n31: class RouterRegistration(BaseModel):\n32:     model_config = ConfigDict(arbitrary_types_allowed=True)\n33:     router: APIRouter\n34:     target: PvfAppTarget\n35: \n36: \n37: class PvfResolvedEntity(BaseModel):\n38:     \"\"\"Result of the application's entity_resolver hook.\"\"\"\n39:     display_name: str | None = None\n40:     customer_id: int | None = None\n41: \n42: \n43: class PvfHookRegistry(BaseModel):\n44:     \"\"\"Hooks connecting pvf internals to application-specific logic.\n45: \n46:     Applications fill these in during startup. All hooks are optional unless a feature\n47:     that needs them is activated in pvf_app_startup.yaml.\n48:     \"\"\"\n49:     model_config = ConfigDict(arbitrary_types_allowed=True)\n50: \n51:     # Entities: resolve an application entity (the thing a share or branding image\n52:     # references) for validation, tenancy checks, and display naming.\n53:     # Signature: (session, usr_context, entity_id) -> PvfResolvedEntity | None\n54:     entity_resolver: Callable | None = None\n55:     # Share links: map a share type (object action) to the outbound email type used for\n56:     # invitations, or None to skip. Signature: (share_type: str) -> str | Enum | None\n57:     share_type_email_dispatcher: Callable | None = None\n58:     # Share links: map a share type to the outbound email type used when a project\n59:     # administrator resends an existing invitation. Signature matches the dispatcher.\n60:     share_resend_email_dispatcher: Callable | None = None\n61:     # Share links: valid access operations per share type. {share_type: [operation, ...]}\n62:     share_action_matrix: dict[str, list[str]] = Field(default_factory=dict)\n63:     # Signature: (session, shares: list) -> dict[int, dict] mapping share id -> extra fields\n67:     share_activity_enricher: Callable | None = None\n68:     # Share links: add application fields to share email template params.\n69:     # Signature: (share_link, base_params: dict) -> dict\n70:     share_email_params_enricher: Callable | None = None\n71:     # External API: per webhook type, build the callback payload and decide readiness.\n72:     # PvfGlobalSettings (active, startup values applied)\n84:     startup_config: Any = None        # PvfStartupConfig as loaded from pvf_app_startup.yaml\n85:     db: Any = None                    # DatabaseConnection (engine already verified)\n86:     app_noauth: FastAPI | None = None\n87:     app_session: FastAPI | None = None\n88:     app_share: FastAPI | None = None\n89:     app_external: FastAPI | None = None\n90:     app_settings: BaseModel | None = None        # live app settings; recommended to have pvf settings merged in\n91:     app_client_settings: BaseModel | None = None  # live default client-session settings instance\n92:     # Informational; surfaced by the root /status endpoint.\n93:     application_name: str | None = None\n94:     application_version: str | None = None\n95:     client_session_context_settings_type: type | None = None\n96: \n97:     routers: dict[PvfAppTarget, list[RouterRegistration]] = Field(\n98:         default_factory=lambda: {target: [] for target in PvfAppTarget}\n99:     )\n100:     openapi_tags: dict[PvfAppTarget, list[dict]] = Field(\n101:         default_factory=lambda: {target: [] for target in PvfAppTarget}\n102:     )\n103:     hooks: PvfHookRegistry = Field(default_factory=PvfHookRegistry)\n104:     schema_extensions: PvfSchemaExtensionRegistry = Field(default_factory=get_schema_extension_registry)\n105: \n106:     def add_router(self, target: PvfAppTarget, router: APIRouter) -> None:\n107:         \"\"\"Register an application router for inclusion in the given FastAPI app.\n108: \n109:         Dependency injection is strictly enforced at composition time by the runner's\n110:         integrity check, regardless of whether the dependency data is an input parameter\n111:         for a given endpoint; see pvf.utils.dependency_integrity.\n112:         \"\"\"\n113:         self.routers[PvfAppTarget(target)].append(RouterRegistration(router=router, target=PvfAppTarget(target)))\n114: \n115:     def target_app(self, target: PvfAppTarget) -> FastAPI:\n116:         return {\n117:             PvfAppTarget.noauth: self.app_noauth,\n118:             PvfAppTarget.session: self.app_session,\n119:             PvfAppTarget.share: self.app_share,\n120:             PvfAppTarget.external: self.app_external,\n121:         }[PvfAppTarget(target)]\n122: \n123: \n124: # The runner records the active invocation here so pvf internals (share gate, webhook\n125: # delivery, ...) can reach registered hooks at request time without importing the app.\n126: invocation_context: PvfInvocation = None\n127: \n128: def set_current_invocation(invocation: PvfInvocation | None) -> None:\n129:     global invocation_context\n130:     invocation_context = invocation\n131: \n132: \n133: def get_current_invocation() -> PvfInvocation | None:\n134:     return invocation_context\n135: \n136: \n137: def get_hooks() -> PvfHookRegistry:\n138:     invocation = invocation_context\n139:     return invocation.hooks if invocation is not None else PvfHookRegistry()\n140: \n141: \n142: def runtime_settings() -> BaseModel:\n143:     \"\"\"Active settings for pvf request paths: the app instance when bound, else pvf_settings.\"\"\"\n144:     invocation = invocation_context\n145:     if invocation is not None and invocation.app_settings is not None:\n146:         return invocation.app_settings\n147:     return pvf_settings\n148: \n\n(End of file - total 148 lines)\n</content>","metadata":{"preview":"\"\"\"PvfInvocation — the binding contract between pvf_app_runner and the application shell.\n\nThe runner builds this object and passes it to the application entry point named in\npvf_app_startup.yaml. The application is expected to:\n  1. create its own settings object (typically subclassing PvfGlobalSettings) and call\n     pvf.config.pvf_startup_config.merge_pvf_settings_into() to receive startup-owned values\n  2. import its model modules (registering table metadata)\n  3. register any hooks connecting framework internals to application logic\n  4. add its routers via add_router() targeted at the appropriate FastAPI app\n  5. optionally extend openapi_tags and set application_version\nThe entry point returns None on success, or an error message string on failure; it may\nalso raise descriptive exceptions to signal startup failure.\n\"\"\"\nfrom enum import StrEnum\nfrom typing import Any, Callable\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, FastAPI\nfrom pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field\n\nfrom ..config.pvf_config_settings import PvfGlobalSettings, pvf_settings","truncated":false,"loaded":[],"display":{"type":"file","path":"/workspaces/powerchoice/src/pvf/bindings/pvf_invocation.py","text":"\"\"\"PvfInvocation — the binding contract between pvf_app_runner and the application shell.\n\nThe runner builds this object and passes it to the application entry point named in\npvf_app_startup.yaml. The application is expected to:\n  1. create its own settings object (typically subclassing PvfGlobalSettings) and call\n     pvf.config.pvf_startup_config.merge_pvf_settings_into() to receive startup-owned values\n  2. import its model modules (registering table metadata)\n  3. register any hooks connecting framework internals to application logic\n  4. add its routers via add_router() targeted at the appropriate FastAPI app\n  5. optionally extend openapi_tags and set application_version\nThe entry point returns None on success, or an error message string on failure; it may\nalso raise descriptive exceptions to signal startup failure.\n\"\"\"\nfrom enum import StrEnum\nfrom typing import Any, Callable\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, FastAPI\nfrom pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field\n\nfrom ..config.pvf_config_settings import PvfGlobalSettings, pvf_settings\nfrom ..utils.pvf_base_internal_resources import PvfClientAuthSettings\n\nclass PvfAppTarget(StrEnum):\n    \"\"\"The four FastAPI applications composed by pvf_app_runner.\"\"\"\n    noauth = \"noauth\"        # no authorization expected: login, password reset, self registration\n    session = \"session\"      # logged-in session (JWT cookie) endpoints\n    share = \"share\"          # share link access (cookie / magic-key gate, no session JWT)\n    external = \"external\"    # external API (signed headers)\n\n\nclass RouterRegistration(BaseModel):\n    model_config = ConfigDict(arbitrary_types_allowed=True)\n    router: APIRouter\n    target: PvfAppTarget\n\n\nclass PvfResolvedEntity(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"Result of the application's entity_resolver hook.\"\"\"\n    display_name: str | None = None\n    customer_id: int | None = None\n\n\nclass PvfHookRegistry(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"Hooks connecting pvf internals to application-specific logic.\n\n    Applications fill these in during startup. All hooks are optional unless a feature\n    that needs them is activated in pvf_app_startup.yaml.\n    \"\"\"\n    model_config = ConfigDict(arbitrary_types_allowed=True)\n\n    # Entities: resolve an application entity (the thing a share or branding image\n    # references) for validation, tenancy checks, and display naming.\n    # Signature: (session, usr_context, entity_id) -> PvfResolvedEntity | None\n    entity_resolver: Callable | None = None\n    # Share links: map a share type (object action) to the outbound email type used for\n    # invitations, or None to skip. Signature: (share_type: str) -> str | Enum | None\n    share_type_email_dispatcher: Callable | None = None\n    # Share links: map a share type to the outbound email type used when a project\n    # administrator resends an existing invitation. Signature matches the dispatcher.\n    share_resend_email_dispatcher: Callable | None = None\n    # Share links: valid access operations per share type. {share_type: [operation, ...]}\n    share_action_matrix: dict[str, list[str]] = Field(default_factory=dict)\n    # Share links: operations that mutate state (require an established cookie session).\n    share_mutating_operations: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)\n    # Share links: enrich activity rows with application stats.\n    # Signature: (session, shares: list) -> dict[int, dict] mapping share id -> extra fields\n    share_activity_enricher: Callable | None = None\n    # Share links: add application fields to share email template params.\n    # Signature: (share_link, base_params: dict) -> dict\n    share_email_params_enricher: Callable | None = None\n    # External API: per webhook type, build the callback payload and decide readiness.\n    # payload_builder signature: (session, event) -> dict\n    # readiness_predicate signature: (session, event) -> bool\n    webhook_payload_builders: dict[str, Callable] = Field(default_factory=dict)\n    webhook_readiness_predicates: dict[str, Callable] = Field(default_factory=dict)\n\n\nfrom ..db.model_factory import PvfSchemaExtensionRegistry, get_schema_extension_registry\n\n\nclass PvfInvocation(BaseModel):\n    model_config = ConfigDict(arbitrary_types_allowed=True)\n    pvf_settings: PvfGlobalSettings = None          # PvfGlobalSettings (active, startup values applied)\n    startup_config: Any = None        # PvfStartupConfig as loaded from pvf_app_startup.yaml\n    db: Any = None                    # DatabaseConnection (engine already verified)\n    app_noauth: FastAPI | None = None\n    app_session: FastAPI | None = None\n    app_share: FastAPI | None = None\n    app_external: FastAPI | None = None\n    app_settings: BaseModel | None = None        # live app settings; recommended to have pvf settings merged in\n    app_client_settings: BaseModel | None = None  # live default client-session settings instance\n    # Informational; surfaced by the root /status endpoint.\n    application_name: str | None = None\n    application_version: str | None = None\n    client_session_context_settings_type: type | None = None\n\n    routers: dict[PvfAppTarget, list[RouterRegistration]] = Field(\n        default_factory=lambda: {target: [] for target in PvfAppTarget}\n    )\n    openapi_tags: dict[PvfAppTarget, list[dict]] = Field(\n        default_factory=lambda: {target: [] for target in PvfAppTarget}\n    )\n    hooks: PvfHookRegistry = Field(default_factory=PvfHookRegistry)\n    schema_extensions: PvfSchemaExtensionRegistry = Field(default_factory=get_schema_extension_registry)\n\n    def add_router(self, target: PvfAppTarget, router: APIRouter) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Register an application router for inclusion in the given FastAPI app.\n\n        Dependency injection is strictly enforced at composition time by the runner's\n        integrity check, regardless of whether the dependency data is an input parameter\n        for a given endpoint; see pvf.utils.dependency_integrity.\n        \"\"\"\n        self.routers[PvfAppTarget(target)].append(RouterRegistration(router=router, target=PvfAppTarget(target)))\n\n    def target_app(self, target: PvfAppTarget) -> FastAPI:\n        return {\n            PvfAppTarget.noauth: self.app_noauth,\n            PvfAppTarget.session: self.app_session,\n            PvfAppTarget.share: self.app_share,\n            PvfAppTarget.external: self.app_external,\n        }[PvfAppTarget(target)]\n\n\n# The runner records the active invocation here so pvf internals (share gate, webhook\n# delivery, ...) can reach registered hooks at request time without importing the app.\ninvocation_context: PvfInvocation = None\n\ndef set_current_invocation(invocation: PvfInvocation | None) -> None:\n    global invocation_context\n    invocation_context = invocation\n\n\ndef get_current_invocation() -> PvfInvocation | None:\n    return invocation_context\n\n\ndef get_hooks() -> PvfHookRegistry:\n    invocation = invocation_context\n    return invocation.hooks if invocation is not None else PvfHookRegistry()\n\n\ndef runtime_settings() -> BaseModel:\n    \"\"\"Active settings for pvf request paths: the app instance when bound, else pvf_settings.\"\"\"\n    invocation = invocation_context\n    if invocation is not None and invocation.app_settings is not None:\n        return invocation.app_settings\n    return pvf_settings\n","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":148,"totalLines":148,"truncated":false}},"title":"src/pvf/bindings/pvf_invocation.py","time":{"start":1787409764095,"end":1787409764185}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_01a029ec88e671e1873336f76412d22a"}},"id":"prt_029ec8937001KbkmSqICUHXULO","sessionID":"ses_fd613a9a0ffeanefk4okv1NNUn","messageID":"msg_029ec7b1f001low5d2WqAIutKH"}}
{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787409764208,"sessionID":"ses_fd613a9a0ffeanefk4okv1NNUn","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"read","callID":"call_01a029ec8c3a7321b8b5abe7efb35a57","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/src/pvf/config/pvf_config_settings.py"},"output":"<path>/workspaces/powerchoice/src/pvf/config/pvf_config_settings.py</path>\n<type>file</type>\n<content>\n1: from typing import Any, ClassVar, Union, Tuple, List\n2: from pydantic import field_validator\n3: from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings, SettingsConfigDict\n4: from os import environ, getpid, getcwd, path\n5: from socket import gethostname\n6: \n7: # TODO: There is an issue under uvicorn with process_name getting unset during initialize, globalsettings must be inadvertently recreated\n8: \n9: # Some scripts run from project root, others run from /src, so we need to hint at the right place for the .env file\n10: pvf_env_dir = path.abspath(getcwd())\n11: if pvf_env_dir.endswith('/src'):\n12:     pvf_env_dir = path.dirname(pvf_env_dir)\n13: \n14: def get_env(type_var, env_var_name, default_value):\n15:     \"\"\" retrieve environment value by env_var_name or its upper case, converted via typ_var, or default value\n16:     \"\"\"\n17:     if env_var_name not in environ:\n18:         env_var_name = env_var_name.upper()\n19:     if env_var_name in environ:\n20:         get_value = environ.get(env_var_name)\n21:         if type_var is not None:\n22:             get_value = type_var(get_value)\n23:     else:\n24:         get_value = default_value\n25:     return get_value\n26: \n27: def parse_sendgrid_template_ids(raw_value: str | None) -> dict[str, str]:\n28:     \"\"\"Parse template_code:sendgrid_id pairs. Format: code_1:d-abc,code_n:d-xyz\"\"\"\n29:     if raw_value is None or not str(raw_value).strip():\n30:         return {}\n31:     template_ids: dict[str, str] = {}\n32:     for pair in raw_value.split(\",\"):\n33:         pair = pair.strip()\n34:         if not pair or \":\" not in pair:\n35:             continue\n36:         code, sg_id = pair.split(\":\", 1)\n37:         code = code.strip()\n38:         sg_id = sg_id.strip()\n39:         if code:\n40:             template_ids[code] = sg_id\n41:     return template_ids\n42: \n43: def parse_csv_name_list(raw_value: str | list | None) -> list[str]:\n44:     \"\"\"Parse a comma-separated name list. Empty string or None becomes []. Already-a-list is stripped.\"\"\"\n45:     if raw_value is None:\n46:         return []\n47:     if isinstance(raw_value, list):\n48:         return [str(token).strip() for token in raw_value if str(token).strip()]\n49:     text = str(raw_value)\n50:     if not text.strip():\n51:         return []\n52:     return [token.strip() for token in text.split(\",\") if token.strip()]\n53: \n54: def get_trial_account_codes_dict(env_var_name, default_value):\n55:     \"\"\" retrieve trial_code_list from default or environment value by env_var_name or its upper case, converted via typ_var, or default value\n56:         format is: code_1:days,code_n:days\n57:     \"\"\"\n58:     if env_var_name not in environ:\n59:         env_var_name = env_var_name.upper()\n60:     if env_var_name in environ:\n61:         get_value = environ.get(env_var_name)\n62:     else:\n63:         get_value = default_value\n64: \n65:     trial_code_list = [x.strip() for x in get_value.split(',') if x.strip() != '']\n66:     trial_code_dict = {}\n67:     for trial_code in trial_code_list:\n68:         trial_code_parts = [x.strip().lower() for x in trial_code.split(':', 2)]\n69:         code = trial_code_parts[0]\n70:         days = int(trial_code_parts[1])\n71:         trial_code_dict[code] = days\n72:     # print('Loaded trial account codes: ', trial_code_dict)\n73:     return trial_code_dict\n74: \n75: class PvfGlobalSettings(BaseSettings):\n76:     PVF_VERSION: str = \"1.2.0\"\n77:     APPLICATION_NAME: str = environ.get(\"APPLICATION_NAME\", \"PV Framework App Name\")\n78: \n79:     model_config = SettingsConfigDict(env_file=path.join(pvf_env_dir, \".env\"))\n80:     server_env: str = \"local\"\n81:     computer_name: str = gethostname()\n82:     environment_name: str = environ.get(\"ENVIRONMENT_NAME\", \"local-dev\")\n83:     process_name: str = \"_not_specified_\"\n84:     process_id: int = getpid()\n85: \n86:     PYTEST_ACTIVE: bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"PYTEST_ACTIVE\", 0)))\n87:     UNIQUE_CONFIGURATION_CHECK: bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"UNIQUE_CONFIGURATION_CHECK\", 0)))  # only set to 1 in local .env, always 0 in example.env, so a check that env is custom\n88: \n89:     APPLICATION_BASE_URL: str = environ.get(\"APPLICATION_BASE_URL\", \"http://localhost:9999\")\n90:     APPLICATION_SERVER_PORT: int = int(environ.get(\"APPLICATION_SERVER_PORT\", 9999))\n91:     APP_LOGIN_PATH: str = environ.get(\"APP_LOGIN_PATH\", \"login\")\n92:     APP_RESET_PASSWORD_URL: str = environ.get(\"APP_RESET_PASSWORD_URL\", \"forgot-password\")\n93: \n94:     # --- Startup-owned values (pvf_app_startup.yaml) -------------------------\n95:     # Deployment-fixed feature switches and share vocabularies. These are set\n96:     # ONLY by the pvf startup logic from pvf_app_startup.yaml (see\n97:     # pvf.config.pvf_startup_config.apply_startup_config). They are declared\n98:     # here as informational dummies; being ClassVars, pydantic-settings will\n99:     # never populate them from .env or process environment, and __setattr__\n100:     # below rejects any ordinary assignment.\n101:     ACTIVATE_STRIPE_INTEGRATION: ClassVar[bool] = False\n102:     ACTIVATE_BRANDING_IMAGE_STORE: ClassVar[bool] = False\n103:     ACTIVATE_SHARE_LINKS: ClassVar[bool] = True\n104:     ACTIVATE_EXTERNAL_API: ClassVar[bool] = True\n105:     ACTIVATE_CUSTOMER_COMMUNICATION: ClassVar[bool] = True\n106:     ACTIVATE_AI_AGENTS: ClassVar[bool] = False\n107:     # Share link vocabularies (normalized to plain string lists at injection).\n108:     # Defaults match the historical powerchoice values so import-time consumers\n109:     # behave unchanged before startup injection runs.\n110:     SHARE_OBJECT_ACTIONS: ClassVar[list[str]] = [\"not_set\", \"vote\", \"vote_view\", \"report\"]\n111:     SHARE_ACCESS_OPERATIONS: ClassVar[list[str]] = [\"not_set\", \"vote\", \"view\", \"report\"]\n112: \n113:     _STARTUP_VALUE_NAMES: ClassVar[frozenset[str]] = frozenset({\n114:         \"ACTIVATE_STRIPE_INTEGRATION\",\n115:         \"ACTIVATE_BRANDING_IMAGE_STORE\",\n116:         \"ACTIVATE_SHARE_LINKS\",\n117:         \"ACTIVATE_EXTERNAL_API\",\n118:         \"ACTIVATE_CUSTOMER_COMMUNICATION\",\n119:         \"ACTIVATE_AI_AGENTS\",\n120:         \"SHARE_OBJECT_ACTIONS\",\n121:         \"SHARE_ACCESS_OPERATIONS\",\n122:     })\n123: \n124:     def __setattr__(self, name: str, value: Any) -> None:\n125:         if name in self._STARTUP_VALUE_NAMES:\n126:             raise RuntimeError(\n127:                 f\"{name} is owned by pvf_app_startup.yaml and is read-only; \"\n128:                 \"only the pvf startup logic may set it\"\n129:             )\n130:         super().__setattr__(name, value)\n131: \n132:     def _inject_startup_values(self, values: dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n133:         \"\"\"Framework startup use only: apply values loaded from pvf_app_startup.yaml.\n134: \n135:         Bypasses the read-only guard deliberately. get_env(type_var, env_var_name, default_value):\n    \"\"\" retrieve environment value by env_var_name or its upper case, converted via typ_var, or default value\n    \"\"\"\n    if env_var_name not in environ:\n        env_var_name = env_var_name.upper()\n    if env_var_name in environ:\n        get_value = environ.get(env_var_name)\n        if type_var is not None:\n            get_value = type_var(get_value)\n    else:\n        get_value = default_value\n    return get_value\n\ndef parse_sendgrid_template_ids(raw_value: str | None) -> dict[str, str]:\n    \"\"\"Parse template_code:sendgrid_id pairs. Format: code_1:d-abc,code_n:d-xyz\"\"\"\n    if raw_value is None or not str(raw_value).strip():\n        return {}\n    template_ids: dict[str, str] = {}\n    for pair in raw_value.split(\",\"):\n        pair = pair.strip()\n        if not pair or \":\" not in pair:\n            continue\n        code, sg_id = pair.split(\":\", 1)\n        code = code.strip()\n        sg_id = sg_id.strip()\n        if code:\n            template_ids[code] = sg_id\n    return template_ids\n\ndef parse_csv_name_list(raw_value: str | list | None) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Parse a comma-separated name list. Empty string or None becomes []. Already-a-list is stripped.\"\"\"\n    if raw_value is None:\n        return []\n    if isinstance(raw_value, list):\n        return [str(token).strip() for token in raw_value if str(token).strip()]\n    text = str(raw_value)\n    if not text.strip():\n        return []\n    return [token.strip() for token in text.split(\",\") if token.strip()]\n\ndef get_trial_account_codes_dict(env_var_name, default_value):\n    \"\"\" retrieve trial_code_list from default or environment value by env_var_name or its upper case, converted via typ_var, or default value\n        format is: code_1:days,code_n:days\n    \"\"\"\n    if env_var_name not in environ:\n        env_var_name = env_var_name.upper()\n    if env_var_name in environ:\n        get_value = environ.get(env_var_name)\n    else:\n        get_value = default_value\n\n    trial_code_list = [x.strip() for x in get_value.split(',') if x.strip() != '']\n    trial_code_dict = {}\n    for trial_code in trial_code_list:\n        trial_code_parts = [x.strip().lower() for x in trial_code.split(':', 2)]\n        code = trial_code_parts[0]\n        days = int(trial_code_parts[1])\n        trial_code_dict[code] = days\n    # print('Loaded trial account codes: ', trial_code_dict)\n    return trial_code_dict\n\nclass PvfGlobalSettings(BaseSettings):\n    PVF_VERSION: str = \"1.2.0\"\n    APPLICATION_NAME: str = environ.get(\"APPLICATION_NAME\", \"PV Framework App Name\")\n\n    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(env_file=path.join(pvf_env_dir, \".env\"))\n    server_env: str = \"local\"\n    computer_name: str = gethostname()\n    environment_name: str = environ.get(\"ENVIRONMENT_NAME\", \"local-dev\")\n    process_name: str = \"_not_specified_\"\n    process_id: int = getpid()\n\n    PYTEST_ACTIVE: bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"PYTEST_ACTIVE\", 0)))\n    UNIQUE_CONFIGURATION_CHECK: bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"UNIQUE_CONFIGURATION_CHECK\", 0)))  # only set to 1 in local .env, always 0 in example.env, so a check that env is custom\n\n    APPLICATION_BASE_URL: str = environ.get(\"APPLICATION_BASE_URL\", \"http://localhost:9999\")\n    APPLICATION_SERVER_PORT: int = int(environ.get(\"APPLICATION_SERVER_PORT\", 9999))\n    APP_LOGIN_PATH: str = environ.get(\"APP_LOGIN_PATH\", \"login\")\n    APP_RESET_PASSWORD_URL: str = environ.get(\"APP_RESET_PASSWORD_URL\", \"forgot-password\")\n\n    # --- Startup-owned values (pvf_app_startup.yaml) -------------------------\n    # Deployment-fixed feature switches and share vocabularies. These are set\n    # ONLY by the pvf startup logic from pvf_app_startup.yaml (see\n    # pvf.config.pvf_startup_config.apply_startup_config). They are declared\n    # here as informational dummies; being ClassVars, pydantic-settings will\n    # never populate them from .env or process environment, and __setattr__\n    # below rejects any ordinary assignment.\n    ACTIVATE_STRIPE_INTEGRATION: ClassVar[bool] = False\n    ACTIVATE_BRANDING_IMAGE_STORE: ClassVar[bool] = False\n    ACTIVATE_SHARE_LINKS: ClassVar[bool] = True\n    ACTIVATE_EXTERNAL_API: ClassVar[bool] = True\n    ACTIVATE_CUSTOMER_COMMUNICATION: ClassVar[bool] = True\n    ACTIVATE_AI_AGENTS: ClassVar[bool] = False\n    # Share link vocabularies (normalized to plain string lists at injection).\n    # Defaults match the historical powerchoice values so import-time consumers\n    # behave unchanged before startup injection runs.\n    SHARE_OBJECT_ACTIONS: ClassVar[list[str]] = [\"not_set\", \"vote\", \"vote_view\", \"report\"]\n    SHARE_ACCESS_OPERATIONS: ClassVar[list[str]] = [\"not_set\", \"vote\", \"view\", \"report\"]\n\n    _STARTUP_VALUE_NAMES: ClassVar[frozenset[str]] = frozenset({\n        \"ACTIVATE_STRIPE_INTEGRATION\",\n        \"ACTIVATE_BRANDING_IMAGE_STORE\",\n        \"ACTIVATE_SHARE_LINKS\",\n        \"ACTIVATE_EXTERNAL_API\",\n        \"ACTIVATE_CUSTOMER_COMMUNICATION\",\n        \"ACTIVATE_AI_AGENTS\",\n        \"SHARE_OBJECT_ACTIONS\",\n        \"SHARE_ACCESS_OPERATIONS\",\n    })\n\n    def __setattr__(self, name: str, value: Any) -> None:\n        if name in self._STARTUP_VALUE_NAMES:\n            raise RuntimeError(\n                f\"{name} is owned by pvf_app_startup.yaml and is read-only; \"\n                \"only the pvf startup logic may set it\"\n            )\n        super().__setattr__(name, value)\n\n    def _inject_startup_values(self, values: dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Framework startup use only: apply values loaded from pvf_app_startup.yaml.\n\n        Bypasses the read-only guard deliberately. Not part of the application-facing API.\n        \"\"\"\n        for name, value in values.items():\n            if name not in self._STARTUP_VALUE_NAMES:\n                raise KeyError(f\"{name} is not a startup-owned setting\")\n            object.__setattr__(self, name, value)\n\n    BOOTSTRAP_SAMPLE_DATA: int = int(environ.get(\"BOOTSTRAP_SAMPLE_DATA\", False))\n    BOOTSTRAP_CUSTOMER_EMAIL: str = environ.get(\"BOOTSTRAP_CUSTOMER_EMAIL\", None)\n    BOOTSTRAP_ADMIN_USER_EMAIL: str = environ.get(\"BOOTSTRAP_ADMIN_USER_EMAIL\", None)\n    BOOTSTRAP_ADMIN_PASSWORD: str = environ.get(\"BOOTSTRAP_ADMIN_PASSWORD\", None)\n    BOOTSTRAP_NON_ADMIN_USER_EMAIL: str = environ.get(\"BOOTSTRAP_NON_ADMIN_USER_EMAIL\", None)\n    BOOTSTRAP_NON_ADMIN_PASSWORD: str = environ.get(\"BOOTSTRAP_NON_ADMIN_PASSWORD\", None)\n\n    APPLICATION_ACCOUNT_GRACE_PERIOD_DAYS: int = int(environ.get(\"APPLICATION_ACCOUNT_GRACE_PERIOD_DAYS\", 3))   # provides this many days after expiration to keep service active\n    APPLICATION_ACCOUNT_TRIAL_GRACE_PERIOD_DAYS: int = int(environ.get(\"APPLICATION_ACCOUNT_TRIAL_GRACE_PERIOD_DAYS\", 3))   # provides this many days after expiration to keep service active\n\n    PLATFORM_LINUX: int = int(environ.get(\"PLATFORM_LINUX\", 1))\n    DB_PATH_OR_CONNECTION_STRING: str = environ.get(\"DB_PATH_OR_CONNECTION_STRING\", \"sqlite:///local.db\")    # Default to creating a local database\n    DB_ECHO_SQL_TO_CONSOLE: bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"DB_ECHO_SQL_TO_CONSOLE\", 0)))\n\n    # Token secrets and settings\n    JWT_SECRET_KEY:str = environ.get(\"JWT_SECRET_KEY\", \"enk1xmalq8pdnkb902x3hzo0uo9ayyoaubt2da57ucvh8pebelvfauyej2bzdak6jieso81ri\")\n    JWT_REFRESH_SECRET_KEY:str = environ.get(\"JWT_REFRESH\", \"12veui23ohkwanuignobggxbws9x5zxs4g8c29fxgb4d3gb7tl7vsz31p9uc3m0al82fitxbgedv\")\n    ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES:int = int(environ.get(\"ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES\", 10080))\n    JWT_ALGORITHM: str = environ.get(\"JWT_ALGORITHM\", \"HS256\")\n    CLEARED_TOKEN_VALUE: str = environ.get(\"CLEARED_TOKEN_VALUE\", \"I'm no longer here, Where did I go?\")\n    PW_RESET_FLOODING_LIMIT_SECONDS: int = int(environ.get(\"PW_RESET_FLOODING_LIMIT_SECONDS\", 90))\n    PW_RESET_TOKEN_VALID_SECONDS: int = int(environ.get(\"PW_RESET_TOKEN_VALID_SECONDS\", 7200))\n    ACCOUNT_ACTIVATION_TOKEN_KEY: str = environ.get(\"ACCOUNT_ACTIVATION_TOKEN_KEY\", \"2b04wgwt4x4xvu2m6u3rfd\")\n\n    # Items with weird values (like TRIAL_ACCOUNT_CODES) need custom field validators to override the default\n    # parser.  Otherwise Pydanting will try to parse this itself with a JSON validator and fail.\n    # The default must be set to None to get the validator to run.\n\n    # TRIAL_ACCOUNT_CODES: dict[str, Tuple[int, int]] = get_trial_account_codes_dict(\"TRIAL_ACCOUNT_CODES\", \"trial_account_30_days:30:100\")  # sample, 30 day trial with 100 tokens granted with same expiration; set with =code:days:tokens,code:days:tokens,... becomes key = (days, tokens)\n    CUSTOMER_ACTIVATION_URL: str = \"activate-customer\"\n    TRIAL_ACCOUNT_CODES: dict[str, int] | None = None  # sample, days of trial for a given trial code. ; example trial_code_1:10,...,trial_code_N:30\n    @field_validator(\"TRIAL_ACCOUNT_CODES\", mode=\"before\")\n    def parse_trial_account_codes(cls, value):\n        \"\"\"Custom parser for TRIAL_ACCOUNT_CODES.\"\"\"\n        if isinstance(value, str):\n            return get_trial_account_codes_dict(\"TRIAL_ACCOUNT_CODES\", value)\n        return get_trial_account_codes_dict(\"TRIAL_ACCOUNT_CODES\", \"trial_account_30_days:30:100\")\n\n    # Comma-separated field names stripped from user_record and customer_record on context/session\n    # responses (multiple values allowed, e.g. stripe_settings,client_settings). Empty string or None\n    # disables masking. Unset uses the default stripe_settings. Converted to list[str] once at load.\n    CONTEXT_MASKED_FIELDS: list[str] | None = None\n    @field_validator(\"CONTEXT_MASKED_FIELDS\", mode=\"before\")\n    def parse_context_masked_fields(cls, value):\n        \"\"\"Custom parser for CONTEXT_MASKED_FIELDS.\"\"\"\n        if value is None:\n            return [\"stripe_settings\"]\n        return parse_csv_name_list(value)\n\n    ACCESS_MAGIC_KEY_EXPIRATION_MINUTES: int = int(environ.get(\"ACCESS_MAGIC_KEY_EXPIRATION_MINUTES\", 70))\n    ACCESS_MAGIC_KEY_EXPIRATION_MESSAGE: str = environ.get(\"ACCESS_MAGIC_KEY_EXPIRATION_MESSAGE\", \"one hour\")\n\n    LOGIN_2FA_MODE: str = environ.get(\"LOGIN_2FA_MODE\", \"disabled\") # disabled / sysadmins / admins / admins+optin / all\n    LOGIN_2FA_VALIDITY_MINUTES: int = int(environ.get(\"LOGIN_2FA_VALIDITY_MINUTES\", 15))\n    LOGIN_2FA_VALIDITY_MESSAGE: str = environ.get(\"LOGIN_2FA_VALIDITY_MESSAGE\", \"15 minutes\")\n    LOGIN_2FA_RESEND_LIMIT_SECONDS: int = int(environ.get(\"LOGIN_2FA_RESEND_LIMIT_SECONDS\", 90))\n    token_security_digits_only: bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"TOKEN_SECURITY_DIGITS_ONLY\", 0)))\n\n    # Share link support (framework-generic; share type/action vocabularies are startup-owned above)\n    VIEW_TOKEN_KEY: str = environ.get(\"VIEW_TOKEN_KEY\", \"view-token-key-change-in-env\")\n    ACCESS_VIEW_COOKIE: str = environ.get(\"ACCESS_VIEW_COOKIE\", \"pc_share_view\")\n    SHARE_LINK_REST_PW_HASH_KEY: str = environ.get(\"SHARE_LINK_REST_PW_HASH_KEY\", \"x6q9uuwmagcy717wioctg6\")\n    SHARE_LINK_EXPIRATION_DAYS: int = int(environ.get(\"SHARE_LINK_EXPIRATION_DAYS\", 30))\n    SHARE_LINK_COOKIE_EXPIRATION_DAYS: int = int(environ.get(\"SHARE_LINK_COOKIE_EXPIRATION_DAYS\", 14))\n    SHARE_LINK_EXPIRATION_MESSAGE: str = environ.get(\"SHARE_LINK_EXPIRATION_MESSAGE\", \"30 days\")\n    SHARE_GATE_THROTTLE_WINDOW_SECONDS: int = int(environ.get(\"SHARE_GATE_THROTTLE_WINDOW_SECONDS\", 3600))\n    SHARE_GATE_MAX_UNAUTH_PER_IP_PER_HOUR: int = int(environ.get(\"SHARE_GATE_MAX_UNAUTH_PER_IP_PER_HOUR\", 60))\n    SHARE_GATE_MAX_FAILURES_PER_IP_PER_HOUR: int = int(environ.get(\"SHARE_GATE_MAX_FAILURES_PER_IP_PER_HOUR\", 20))\n    SHARE_GATE_MAX_FAILURES_PER_TOKEN_PER_HOUR: int = int(environ.get(\"SHARE_GATE_MAX_FAILURES_PER_TOKEN_PER_HOUR\", 10))\n\n    SHARE_PROJECT_APP_VIEW_BASE_URL: str = \"/share/\"   # must match client share landing routes\n    SHARE_PROJECT_APP_VIEW_SUFFIX_URL: str = \"\"   # optional suffix after magic token\n    SHARE_WS_BASE_URL: str = \"/ext-ws/share/\"  # API path prefix used for share cookies\n    # Security notes included in share invitation emails (plain text guidance for recipients)\n    SHARE_SECURITY_PASSWORD_INCLUDED_NOTE: str = \"A password is required to open this link. Password: {share_password}\"\n    SHARE_SECURITY_PASSWORD_SEPARATE_NOTE: str = \"A password is required to open this link. The password was provided separately.\"\n    SHARE_SECURITY_NOTE_OPEN_ACCESS: str = \"This link is open access; no additional verification is required.\"\n    SHARE_SECURITY_NOTE_EMAIL_ANY_UNVERIFIED: str = \"You will be asked to enter an email address to continue.\"\n    SHARE_SECURITY_NOTE_EMAIL_ANY_VERIFIED: str = \"You will verify any email address via a one-time access key.\"\n    SHARE_SECURITY_NOTE_EMAIL_MATCHING: str = \"You must enter the email address this link was sent to.\"\n    SHARE_SECURITY_NOTE_EMAIL_MATCHING_VERIFIED: str = \"You must verify the email address this link was sent to via a one-time access key.\"\n    SHARE_SECURITY_NOTE_RECIPIENT_EMAIL_VERIFIED: str = \"A one-time access key will be sent to the email on file for this link.\"\n    SHARE_SECURITY_NOTE_PASSWORD_ONLY: str = \"A password is required to open this link.\"\n    SHARE_SECURITY_NOTE_PASSWORD_WITH_EMAIL_ANY_UNVERIFIED: str = \"A password and an email address are required to open this link.\"\n    SHARE_SECURITY_NOTE_PASSWORD_WITH_EMAIL_ANY_VERIFIED: str = \"A password is required, then any email is verified via a one-time access key.\"\n    SHARE_SECURITY_NOTE_PASSWORD_WITH_EMAIL_MATCHING: str = \"A password and the original recipient email are required.\"\n    SHARE_SECURITY_NOTE_PASSWORD_WITH_EMAIL_MATCHING_VERIFIED: str = \"A password is required, then the original recipient email is verified via a one-time access key.\"\n    SHARE_SECURITY_NOTE_PASSWORD_WITH_RECIPIENT_EMAIL_VERIFIED: str = \"A password is required, then a one-time access key is sent to the email on file.\"\n\n    # UI option gating for share access modes. These flags limit the choices offered in the\n    # client only; the backend gate supports every access mode regardless, so an application\n    # can tailor options to its audience without disabling functionality.\n    ENABLE_SHARE_OPEN_ACCESS : bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"ENABLE_SHARE_OPEN_ACCESS\", 0)))                      # Access Url; Enter Name; Access granted.\n    ENABLE_SHARE_EMAIL_ANY_UNVERIFIED : bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"ENABLE_SHARE_EMAIL_ANY_UNVERIFIED\", 1)))   # Access Url; Enter Name & Any Email (not verified); Access Granted.\n    ENABLE_SHARE_EMAIL_ANY_VERIFIED : bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"ENABLE_SHARE_EMAIL_ANY_VERIFIED\", 1)))       # Access Url; Enter Name & Any Email; Token email sent; Follow link or enter key from token email; Access Granted.\n    ENABLE_SHARE_EMAIL_MATCHING : bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"ENABLE_SHARE_EMAIL_MATCHING\", 1)))               # Access Url; Enter Name & Email; Email must match share; Access Granted.\n    ENABLE_SHARE_EMAIL_MATCHING_VERIFIED : bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"ENABLE_SHARE_EMAIL_MATCHING_VERIFIED\", 0)))   # Access Url; Enter Name & Email; Email must match share; Token email sent; Use link or key to verify; Access Granted.\n    ENABLE_SHARE_RECIPIENT_EMAIL_VERIFIED : bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"ENABLE_SHARE_RECIPIENT_EMAIL_VERIFIED\", 1))) # Access Url; Enter Name Only; Token email sent (based on share value); Use link or key to verify; Access Granted.\n    ENABLE_SHARE_PASSWORD_ONLY : bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"ENABLE_SHARE_PASSWORD_ONLY\", 0)))                        # Access Url; Enter Name Only; Password required from share; Access Granted.\n    ENABLE_SHARE_PASSWORD_WITH_EMAIL_ANY_UNVERIFIED : bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"ENABLE_SHARE_PASSWORD_WITH_EMAIL_ANY_UNVERIFIED\", 1)))   # Access Url; Enter Name & Any Email (not verified); Password required from share; Access Granted.\n    ENABLE_SHARE_PASSWORD_WITH_EMAIL_ANY_VERIFIED : bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"ENABLE_SHARE_PASSWORD_WITH_EMAIL_ANY_VERIFIED\", 0)))       # Access Url; Enter Name & Any Email (verified); Password required from share; Token email sent; Use link or key to verify; Access Granted.\n    ENABLE_SHARE_PASSWORD_WITH_EMAIL_MATCHING: bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"ENABLE_SHARE_PASSWORD_WITH_EMAIL_MATCHING\", 0)))          # Access Url; Enter Name & Email; Email must match share; Password required from share; Access Granted.\n    ENABLE_SHARE_PASSWORD_WITH_EMAIL_MATCHING_VERIFIED : bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"ENABLE_SHARE_PASSWORD_WITH_EMAIL_MATCHING_VERIFIED\", 0)))   # Access Url; Enter Name & Email; Email must match share; Password required from share; Token email sent; Use link or key to verify; Access Granted.\n    ENABLE_SHARE_PASSWORD_WITH_RECIPIENT_EMAIL_VERIFIED : bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"ENABLE_SHARE_PASSWORD_WITH_RECIPIENT_EMAIL_VERIFIED\", 0))) # Access Url; Enter Name Only; Password required from share; Token email sent (based on share value); Use link or key to verify; Access Granted.\n\n    # External (customer toolkit) API signing\n    CUSTOMER_API_INBOUND_HASH_KEY: str = environ.get(\"CUSTOMER_API_INBOUND_HASH_KEY\", \"265c0a4a9832bcd2c7ffd8eb89dc11ca\")\n    CUSTOMER_API_OUTBOUND_HASH_KEY: str = environ.get(\"CUSTOMER_API_OUTBOUND_HASH_KEY\", \"7aa7b7a445b96cdf2f5c701854b8af9c\")\n    CUSTOMER_API_TIME_TOLERANCE: int = int(environ.get(\"CUSTOMER_API_TIME_TOLERANCE\", 4800))   # 80 minutes\n\n    # Watcher process (callback delivery and other background modules)\n    WATCHER_PROCESS_MODULE_LIST: list[str] = [x.strip() for x in environ.get(\"WATCHER_PROCESS_MODULE_LIST\", \"\").split(',')]\n    WATCHER_PROCESS_SHOW_API_CALLBACKS: bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"WATCHER_PROCESS_SHOW_API_CALLBACKS\", 0)))  # if true, the watcher will display API callback summary as processed\n    WATCHER_PROCESS_API_CALLBACKS: bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"WATCHER_PROCESS_API_CALLBACKS\", 1)))  # if true, the watcher will process and deliver eligible API callbacks\n    WATCHER_PROCESS_API_CALLBACKS_TAG: str = environ.get(\"WATCHER_PROCESS_API_CALLBACKS_TAG\", \"process_api_callbacks\")  # tag for surrogate module types used in the watcher\n    WATCHER_PROCESS_API_CALLBACKS_MAXIMUM_ATTEMPTS: int = int(environ.get(\"WATCHER_PROCESS_API_CALLBACKS_MAXIMUM_ATTEMPTS\", 5))  # maximum number of attempts to deliver an API callback before it is considered failed\n    WATCHER_PROCESS_API_CALLBACKS_RETRY_DELAYS: list[int] = [int(x.strip()) for x in environ.get(\"WATCHER_PROCESS_API_CALLBACKS_RETRY_DELAYS\", \"1,4,10,60,60,120,120,120,120,120\").split(',')]\n    WATCHER_SHORTER_BUSY_POLL_SLEEP: float = float(environ.get(\"WATCHER_SHORTER_BUSY_POLL_SLEEP\", 1.0))  # seconds between polls while work is being found\n    WATCHER_LONGER_IDLE_INCREASING_POLL_SLEEP: float = float(environ.get(\"WATCHER_LONGER_IDLE_INCREASING_POLL_SLEEP\", 5.0))  # base seconds between polls once idle; grows by the exponential factor\n    WATCHER_POLL_EXPONENTIAL_FACTOR: float = float(environ.get(\"WATCHER_POLL_EXPONENTIAL_FACTOR\", 1.2))  # idle poll backoff multiplier\n    WATCHER_POLL_MAXIMUM_SLEEP_TIME: float = float(environ.get(\"WATCHER_POLL_MAXIMUM_SLEEP_TIME\", 30.0))  # cap on seconds between polls\n    WATCHER_PROCESS_EMAIL_LISTENER_ENABLE: bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"WATCHER_PROCESS_EMAIL_LISTENER_ENABLE\", 0)))  # reserved; no email listener processing is implemented\n    WATCHER_PROCESS_EMAIL_LISTENER_TAG: str = environ.get(\"WATCHER_PROCESS_EMAIL_LISTENER_TAG\", \"process_email_listener\")  # tag for surrogate module types used for email listener\n    WATCHER_PROCESS_AI_AGENTS_TAG: str = environ.get(\"WATCHER_PROCESS_AI_AGENTS_TAG\", \"process_ai_agents\")\n    WATCHER_EMAIL_LISTENER_SUBMISSION_ADDRESS: str = environ.get(\"WATCHER_EMAIL_LISTENER_SUBMISSION_ADDRESS\", \"\")\n\n    MAX_REPORTS_PER_DAY: int = int(environ.get(\"MAX_REPORTS_PER_DAY\", 8))\n\n    # \n    USE_EMAIL_SERVICE: str | None = environ.get(\"USE_EMAIL_SERVICE\", None) # None disables outbound email; SMTP-JINJA uses local templates and defined SMTP; SENDGRID uses sendgrid settings, account, and templates\n    EMAIL_LOCAL_ALLOW_DOMAINS: str = environ.get(\"EMAIL_LOCAL_ALLOW_DOMAINS\", \"mailinator.com\")\n    EMAIL_BRAND_NAME: str = environ.get(\"EMAIL_BRAND_NAME\", \"Power Choice Pro\")\n\n    EMAIL_SUPPORT_EMAIL: str = environ.get(\"EMAIL_SUPPORT_EMAIL\", \"\")\n    MAX_EMAILS_PER_DEST_PER_HOUR: int = int(environ.get(\"MAX_EMAILS_PER_DEST_PER_HOUR\", 4))\n    MAX_EMAILS_PER_DEST_PER_24_HOURS: int = int(environ.get(\"MAX_EMAILS_PER_DEST_PER_24_HOURS\", 8))\n    MAX_EMAILS_PER_REMOTE_IP_PER_HOUR_VERIFIED: int = int(environ.get(\"MAX_EMAILS_PER_REMOTE_IP_PER_HOUR_VERIFIED\", 20))\n    MAX_EMAILS_PER_REMOTE_IP_PER_HOUR: int = int(environ.get(\"MAX_EMAILS_PER_REMOTE_IP_PER_HOUR\", 4))\n\n    # Settings to use a direct SMTP sender.\n    DIRECT_EMAIL_FROM_NAME: str = environ.get(\"DIRECT_EMAIL_FROM_NAME\", 'Service')\n    DIRECT_EMAIL_FROM_ADDRESS: str = environ.get(\"DIRECT_EMAIL_FROM_ADDRESS\", 'no_reply@domain.com')\n    DIRECT_EMAIL_SMTP_HOST: str = environ.get(\"DIRECT_EMAIL_SMTP_HOST\", \"smtp.example.com\")\n    DIRECT_EMAIL_SMTP_PORT: int = int(environ.get(\"DIRECT_EMAIL_SMTP_PORT\", 587)) # often 465 for SSL\n    DIRECT_EMAIL_SMTP_TLS: bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"DIRECT_EMAIL_SMTP_TLS\", 1)))\n    DIRECT_EMAIL_SMTP_SSL: bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"DIRECT_EMAIL_SMTP_SSL\", 1)))\n    DIRECT_EMAIL_SMTP_USER: str = environ.get(\"DIRECT_EMAIL_SMTP_USER\", \"\")\n    DIRECT_EMAIL_SMTP_PASSWORD: str = environ.get(\"DIRECT_EMAIL_SMTP_PASSWORD\", \"\")\n    DIRECT_EMAIL_SMTP_TIMEOUT: int = int(environ.get(\"DIRECT_EMAIL_SMTP_TIMEOUT\", 20))\n    DIRECT_TEMPLATE_DIRECTORY: str = environ.get(\"DIRECT_TEMPLATE_DIRECTORY\", \"../email_templates\")\n\n    #
