UI focused API for single page application and specialized applications.\",\n        \"externalDocs\": {\n            \"description\": \"See Also: Toolkit Request API Documentation Endpoint\",\n            \"url\": \"{base_url}/api/docs\",\n        },\n    },\n    {\"name\": \"manage-projects\", \"description\": \"Services to manage customer defined projects\"},\n    {\"name\": \"manage-project-content\", \"description\": \"Services to manage project alternatives, factors/criteria, and factor templates\"},\n    {\"name\": \"api-keys\", \"description\": \"Services to manage customer assigned API keys bound to applications\"},","truncated":false,"loaded":[],"display":{"type":"file","path":"/workspaces/powerchoice/src/app_shell.py","text":"\"\"\"PowerChoice application shell — the bridge between pvf_app_runner and the application.\n\nNamed in pvf_app_startup.yaml (application.shell_module / application.entry_point).\nThe entry point receives the PvfInvocation, wires application settings, models, hooks,\nand routers into it, and returns None on success (or an error message on failure).\n\"\"\"\nfrom .pvf.bindings.pvf_invocation import PvfAppTarget, PvfInvocation\n\n_OPENAPI_TAGS_SESSION = [\n    {\n        \"name\": \"powerchoice_session_api\",\n        \"description\": \"Session Based web service operations for **PowerChoice Service** - UI focused API for single page application and specialized applications.\",\n        \"externalDocs\": {\n            \"description\": \"See Also: Toolkit Request API Documentation Endpoint\",\n            \"url\": \"{base_url}/api/docs\",\n        },\n    },\n    {\"name\": \"manage-projects\", \"description\": \"Services to manage customer defined projects\"},\n    {\"name\": \"manage-project-content\", \"description\": \"Services to manage project alternatives, factors/criteria, and factor templates\"},\n    {\"name\": \"api-keys\", \"description\": \"Services to manage customer assigned API keys bound to applications\"},\n    {\"name\": \"dashboard\", \"description\": \"Services to access the general dashboard purposes\"},\n    {\"name\": \"share\", \"description\": \"Authenticated services to create and manage project share links\"},\n    {\"name\": \"project-votes\", \"description\": \"Logged-in voting sessions, observations, and project report analytics\"},\n    {\"name\": \"user-communication\", \"description\": \"User Communication — submit and review bug reports and suggestions\"},\n    {\"name\": \"deprecated\", \"description\": \"Calls that are pending deprecation - see notes for alternatives\"},\n    {\"name\": \"test-helpers\", \"description\": \"Testing only. Endpoints used by automated and e2e tests; not registered in production.\"},\n]\n\n_OPENAPI_TAGS_SHARE = [\n    {\"name\": \"share_ext\", \"description\": \"External (cookie/magic-key) access to shared project vote, status, and report views\"},\n]\n\n_OPENAPI_TAGS_EXTERNAL = [\n    {\n        \"name\": \"powerchoice_toolkit_api\",\n        \"description\": \"Toolkit Request API access to PowerChoice Service\",\n        \"externalDocs\": {\n            \"description\": \"See Also: Session Based API Documentation Endpoint\",\n            \"url\": \"{base_url}/session/docs\",\n        },\n    },\n    {\"name\": \"general\", \"description\": \"Retrieve defined projects and other toolkit details\"},\n    {\"name\": \"callback_examples\", \"description\": \"Examples of callback payload configurations\"},\n]\n\n\ndef register_app_hooks(hooks) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Register PowerChoice hooks on a PvfHookRegistry.\n\n    Called by app_startup (server process) and by the watcher process, which runs\n    separately and needs the same application bindings (webhook payload builders, etc.).\n    \"\"\"\n    from .utils.base_classes_and_enums import OutboundEmailType\n    from .db.models.customer_projects import CustomerProject\n    from .db.models.project_vote_events import ProjectVoteParticipant\n    from .pvf.bindings.pvf_invocation import PvfResolvedEntity\n\n    def powerchoice_entity_resolver(session, usr_context, entity_id: int) -> PvfResolvedEntity | None:\n        \"\"\"Resolve a CustomerProject for pvf share/branding validation, tenancy, and naming.\"\"\"\n        customer_id = usr_context.sess_user.customer_id if usr_context and usr_context.sess_user else None\n        if customer_id is None:\n            return None\n        project = CustomerProject.get_customer_project_by_id_system(session=session, customer_id=customer_id, id=entity_id, clear_lock=False)\n        if project is None:\n            return None\n        return PvfResolvedEntity(display_name=project.project_title or project.project_tag, customer_id=project.customer_id)\n\n    _SHARE_EMAIL_TYPES = {\n        \"vote\": OutboundEmailType.share_project_vote,\n        \"vote_view\": OutboundEmailType.share_project_vote_view,\n        \"report\": OutboundEmailType.share_project_report,\n    }\n\n    def share_activity_enricher(session, usr_context, shares: list) -> dict[int, dict]:\n        \"\"\"Participant/observation counts per share for the activity endpoints.\"\"\"\n        from sqlmodel import select\n        from sqlalchemy import func\n\n        stats: dict[int, dict] = {}\n        share_ids = [share.id for share in shares]\n        if not share_ids:\n            return stats\n        for share_id in share_ids:\n            stats[int(share_id)] = {\"participant_count\": 0, \"observation_count\": 0}\n        vote_rows = session.exec(\n            select(\n                ProjectVoteParticipant.share_id,\n                func.count(ProjectVoteParticipant.id),\n                func.coalesce(func.sum(ProjectVoteParticipant.comparison_count), 0),\n            )\n            .where(\n                ProjectVoteParticipant.customer_id == usr_context.sess_user.customer_id,\n                ProjectVoteParticipant.share_id.in_(share_ids),\n                ProjectVoteParticipant.deleted_date.is_(None),\n            )\n            .group_by(ProjectVoteParticipant.share_id)\n        ).all()\n        for share_id, part_count, obs_sum in vote_rows:\n            if share_id is not None:\n                stats[int(share_id)] = {\"participant_count\": int(part_count or 0), \"observation_count\": int(obs_sum or 0)}\n        return stats\n\n    hooks.entity_resolver = powerchoice_entity_resolver\n    hooks.share_type_email_dispatcher = _SHARE_EMAIL_TYPES.get\n    hooks.share_resend_email_dispatcher = lambda _share_type: OutboundEmailType.resending_invitation\n    hooks.share_action_matrix = {\n        share_type: [\"vote\", \"view\", \"report\"]\n        for share_type in (\"vote\", \"vote_view\", \"report\")\n    }\n    hooks.share_mutating_operations = [\"vote\"]\n    hooks.share_activity_enricher = share_activity_enricher\n\n\ndef app_startup(invocation: PvfInvocation) -> str | None:\n    \"\"\"PowerChoice startup entry point called by pvf_app_runner with the PvfInvocation.\"\"\"\n    # 1. application settings (subclass of PvfGlobalSettings) + startup value merge\n    from .config.config_settings import settings\n    from .config.config_client import ClientSettings, client_settings\n    from .pvf.bindings.pvf_startup_config import merge_pvf_settings_into\n\n    merge_pvf_settings_into(settings)\n    invocation.app_settings = settings\n    invocation.app_client_settings = client_settings\n    invocation.client_session_context_settings_type = ClientSettings\n    invocation.application_name = settings.APPLICATION_NAME\n    invocation.application_version = settings.VERSION\n    \n    # 2. register application table metadata (also the alembic probe models_module)\n    from .db.models import bootstrap  # noqa: F401\n\n    # 3. application startup tasks: seed system shared templates; sample data outside prod\n    from .pvf.bindings.pvf_services import get_next_session, log_event, populate_test_customer_and_user\n    from .utils.bootstrap_system_templates import bootstrap_system_shared_templates\n\n    try:\n        with get_next_session() as session:\n            bootstrap_system_shared_templates(session)\n    except Exception as ex:\n        log_event(\n            f\"app_startup: bootstrap_system_shared_templates failed: {ex}\",\n            severity=2,\n            ex_info=ex,\n        )\n\n    if settings.is_prod() is False and settings.BOOTSTRAP_SAMPLE_DATA:\n        populate_test_customer_and_user(get_next_session())\n\n    # 4. application hooks binding pvf internals to PowerChoice logic\n    register_app_hooks(invocation.hooks)\n\n    # 5. application routers targeted at the appropriate FastAPI apps\n    from .api.app_customer_project_management import router as app_customer_project_router\n    from .api.app_customer_project_content_management import router as app_customer_project_content_router\n    from .api.app_dashboard_views import router as app_entries_views_router\n    from .api.app_shared_link_ext_access import router as app_shared_link_ext_access_router\n    from .api.app_project_vote_events import router as app_project_vote_events_router\n    from .api.api_probe_customer_status import router as api_probe_services_router\n\n    invocation.add_router(PvfAppTarget.session, app_customer_project_router)\n    invocation.add_router(PvfAppTarget.session, app_customer_project_content_router)\n    invocation.add_router(PvfAppTarget.session, app_entries_views_router)\n    invocation.add_router(PvfAppTarget.session, app_project_vote_events_router)\n    invocation.add_router(PvfAppTarget.share, app_shared_link_ext_access_router)\n    invocation.add_router(PvfAppTarget.external, api_probe_services_router)\n\n    # Testing only. from src.config.config_settings import settings\n2: from src.pvf.config.pvf_config_settings import parse_csv_name_list\n3: from src.pvf.db.models.customer_user import Customer\n4: from src.pvf.depends.api_session_dependencies import get_next_session\n5: from src.tests.helpers.auth_client import as_user, clear_auth\n6: \n7: _STRIPE_SETTINGS_BLOB = '{\"livemode\": false, \"secret\": \"billing-internal\"}'\n8: \n9: \n10: def _persist_customer_stripe_settings(customer_id: int, blob: str) -> None:\n11:     session = get_next_session()\n12:     customer = Customer.get_customer_by_id_system(session=session, id=customer_id, clear_lock=False)\n13:     customer.stripe_settings = blob\n14:     customer.update_customer_system(session=session, clear_lock=True)\n15: \n16: \n17: def _read_customer_stripe_settings(customer_id: int) -> str | None:\n18:     session = get_next_session()\n19:     customer = Customer.get_customer_by_id_system(session=session, id=customer_id, clear_lock=True)\n20:     return customer.stripe_settings\n21: \n22: \n23: def test_parse_csv_name_list_variants():\n24:     assert parse_csv_name_list(\"stripe_settings, client_settings\") == [\"stripe_settings\", \"client_settings\"]\n25:     assert parse_csv_name_list(\"\") == []\n26:     assert parse_csv_name_list(None) == []\n27:     assert parse_csv_name_list(\"   \") == []\n28:     assert parse_csv_name_list(\"  a, , b ,\") == [\"a\", \"b\"]\n29:     assert parse_csv_name_list([\" stripe_settings \", \"\", \"x\"]) == [\"stripe_settings\", \"x\"]\n30: \n31: \n32: def test_context_masked_fields_validator_default_and_empty():\n33:     assert settings.parse_context_masked_fields(None) == [\"stripe_settings\"]\n34: self.sess_user.power_user_mode = 0\n            self.sess_user.customer_admin = 0\n            self.sess_user.system_user_mode = 0\n            return\n        \nclass UserSpec(SQLModel):\n    id: int UserResult_One(failure_reason=failure_reason, log_id=log_id)\n        return self.create_user_system(session, password, customer_id=usr_context.sess_user.customer_id, clear_lock=clear_lock)\n\n    def create_user_system(self, session: Session, password: str, customer_id: int=None, clear_lock: bool=True) -> UserResult_One:\n        if self.id not in (-1, 0, None):\n            existing_user = existing_user_result\n        existing_user = existing_user_result.user_info\n        failure_reason, log_id, existing_user  = User.check_user_access_to_user('update_user', key=self.id, usr_context=usr_context, check_user=existing_user, update_mode=True)\n        if existing_user is None or failure_reason != '':\n            return UserResult_One(failure_reason=failure_reason, log_id=log_id)\n        if self.customer_admin is False and existing_user.customer_admin is True:\n            admin_results = User.get_account_admins(session, usr_context=usr_context)\n            admin_users_list = admin_results.user_info_list\n            if failure_reason or len(admin_users_list) < 2:\n                log_id = log_event(log_message=f'Cannot remove the only admin user {existing_user.email} ({existing_user.id})', severity=3, \n                                   usr_context=usr_context)\n                return UserResult_One(failure_reason='Cannot remove the only session.commit()\n        session.refresh(existing_user)\n        if new_password is not None:\n            existing_pw_row = session.exec(select(UserPasswords).where(UserPasswords.user_id == session.add(existing_pw_row)  # performing an update\n            session.commit()\n        if clear_lock: session.close()\n        return UserResult_One(user_info=return_user)\n    \n    def clear_lock: session.close()\n            return False\n        pwd_context = CryptContext(schemes=[\"pbkdf2_sha512\"], deprecated=\"auto\")\n        hashed_password = pwd_context.hash(new_password)\n @staticmethod\n    def get_account_users(session: Session, *, usr_context: UserContext, clear_lock: bool=True) -> UserResult_Many:\n        # note - any account user can see other users, just no created_date=account_user_row.create_date))\n    return context_view\n\n\n@router.post(\n    \"/core/update-customer-vote-settings\",\n    summary=\"Admin: update customer-level compare/results settings\",\n    tags=[\"context\"],\n)\nasync def update_customer_vote_settings(\n    session: SessionDep,\n    usr_context: UserAccessDep,\n    form: UpdateCustomerVoteSettingsForm,\n) -> CustomerVoteSettingsResult:\n    if not usr_context.sess_user.customer_admin:\n        log_id = log_event(\n            \"Non-admin attempted to update customer vote settings\",\n            usr_context=usr_context,\n            severity=3,\n        )\n        return CustomerVoteSettingsResult(failure_reason=\"Customer admin access required\", log_id=log_id)\n\n    customer = usr_context.sess_customer\n    if customer is None:\n        customer = Customer.get_customer_by_id_system(\n            session=session, id=usr_context.sess_user.customer_id, clear_lock=False\n        )\n    if customer is None:\n        return CustomerVoteSettingsResult(failure_reason=\"Customer not found\")\n\n    data = _parse_settings_blob(customer.client_settings)\n    # Strip retired SoftMax / triple-frame knobs\n    if form.coherence_method is not None:\n        m = str(form.coherence_method).strip().lower()\n        data[\"coherence_method\"] = m if m in (\"spearman\", \"kendall\") else \"spearman\"\n    customer.client_settings = json.dumps(data) if data else None\n    customer.update_customer_system(session=session, clear_lock=False)\n    merged = _merged_client_settings(customer.client_settings, usr_context.sess_user.client_settings)\n    apply_2fa_client_settings(merged, usr_context.sess_user, customer)\n    return CustomerVoteSettingsResult(settings=merged)\n\n\nclass CustomerAiSettingsForm(BaseModel):\n    ai_provider: str | None = Field(default=None, description=\"opencode_go, opencode_zen, or openrouter\")\n    ai_model: str | None = Field(default=None, description=\"Default model identifier for this customer\")\n    ai_api_key: str | None = Field(default=None, description=\"Authorization key; send stored encoded value to leave unchanged, blank to clear\")\n\n\nclass CustomerAiSettingsResult(WsResultPackage):\n    ai_provider: str | None = None\n    ai_model: str | None = None\n    ai_api_key: str | None = None\n    key_configured: bool = False\n    system_provider_configured: bool = False\n    providers: list[str] = Field(default_factory=lambda: list(SUPPORTED_LLM_PROVIDERS))\n\n\nclass TestCustomerAiConnectionForm(BaseModel):\n    ai_provider: str\n    ai_api_key: str | None = None\n    ai_model: str | None = None\n\n\nclass TestCustomerAiConnectionResult(WsResultPackage):\n    ok: bool = False\n    models: list[dict] = Field(default_factory=list)\n    provider: str | None = None\n\n\nclass LlmAvailableModelsResult(WsResultPackage):\n    source: str | None = None\n    provider: str | None = None\n    default_model: str | None = None\n    default_is_system: bool = False\n    models: list[dict] = Field(default_factory=list)\n\n\ndef _require_customer_admin(usr_context) -> str | None:\n    if not usr_context.sess_user.customer_admin:\n        log_event(\n            \"Non-admin attempted to update customer AI settings\",\n            usr_context=usr_context,\n            severity=3,\n        )\n        return \"Customer admin access required\"\n    return None\n\n\ndef _load_session_customer(session: Session, usr_context) -> Customer | None:\n    customer = usr_context.sess_customer\n    if customer is None:\n        customer = Customer.get_customer_by_id_system(\n            session=session, id=usr_context.sess_user.customer_id, clear_lock=False\n        )\n    return customer\n\n\ndef _ai_settings_view(customer: Customer) -> CustomerAiSettingsResult:\n    stored_key = customer.ai_api_key or \"\"\n    return CustomerAiSettingsResult(\n        ai_provider=customer.ai_provider or None,\n        ai_model=customer.ai_model or None,\n        ai_api_key=stored_key,\n        key_configured=bool(customer.decrypted_ai_api_key().strip()),\n        system_provider_configured=system_llm_configured(),\n        providers=list(SUPPORTED_LLM_PROVIDERS),\n    )\n\n\ndef _resolve_submitted_key(customer: Customer, submitted: str | None) -> str:\n    value = \"\" if submitted is None else str(submitted)\n    stored = customer.ai_api_key or \"\"\n    if value == stored or is_stored_secret(value):\n        return customer.decrypted_ai_api_key()\n    return value\n\n\n@router.get(\n    \"/core/customer-ai-settings\",\n    summary=\"Admin: read customer LLM provider, model, and encoded key\",\n    tags=[\"context\"],\n)\nasync def get_customer_ai_settings(\n    session: SessionDep,\n    usr_context: UserAccessDep,\n) -> CustomerAiSettingsResult:\n    if not _ai_yaml_activated():\n        return CustomerAiSettingsResult(failure_reason=\"AI features are not enabled\")\n    denied = _require_customer_admin(usr_context)\n    if denied:\n        return CustomerAiSettingsResult(failure_reason=denied)\n    customer = _load_session_customer(session, usr_context)\n    if customer is None:\n        return CustomerAiSettingsResult(failure_reason=\"Customer not found\")\n    return _ai_settings_view(customer)\n\n\n@router.post(\n    \"/core/customer-ai-settings\",\n    summary=\"Admin: update customer LLM provider, model, and authorization key\",\n    tags=[\"context\"],\n)\nasync def update_customer_ai_settings(\n    session: SessionDep,\n    usr_context: UserAccessDep,\n    form: CustomerAiSettingsForm,\n) -> CustomerAiSettingsResult:\n    if not _ai_yaml_activated():\n        return CustomerAiSettingsResult(failure_reason=\"AI features are not enabled\")\n    denied = _require_customer_admin(usr_context)\n    if denied:\n        return CustomerAiSettingsResult(failure_reason=denied)\n    customer = _load_session_customer(session, usr_context)\n    if customer is None:\n        return CustomerAiSettingsResult(failure_reason=\"Customer not found\")\n    provider = str(form.ai_provider or \"\").strip()\n    if provider:\n        provider = normalize_llm_provider(provider)\n        if not provider_is_supported(provider):\n            return CustomerAiSettingsResult(failure_reason=\"Unsupported LLM provider\")\n        customer.ai_provider = provider\n    else:\n        customer.ai_provider = None\n    customer.ai_model = (str(form.ai_model or \"\").strip() or None)\n    customer.apply_ai_api_key_update(form.ai_api_key)\n    customer.update_customer_system(session=session, clear_lock=False)\n    return _ai_settings_view(customer)\n\n\n@router.post(\n    \"/core/test-customer-ai-connection\",\n    summary=\"Admin: test LLM provider credentials and list available models\",\n    tags=[\"context\"],\n)\nasync def test_customer_ai_connection(\n    session: SessionDep,\n    usr_context: UserAccessDep,\n    form: TestCustomerAiConnectionForm,\n) -> TestCustomerAiConnectionResult:\n    if not _ai_yaml_activated():\n        return TestCustomerAiConnectionResult(failure_reason=\"AI features are not enabled\")\n    denied = _require_customer_admin(usr_context)\n    if denied:\n        return TestCustomerAiConnectionResult(failure_reason=denied)\n    customer = _load_session_customer(session, usr_context)\n    if customer is None:\n        return TestCustomerAiConnectionResult(failure_reason=\"Customer not found\")\n    provider = normalize_llm_provider(form.ai_provider)\n    if not provider_is_supported(provider):\n        return TestCustomerAiConnectionResult(failure_reason=\"Unsupported LLM provider\")\n    key = _resolve_submitted_key(customer, form.ai_api_key)\n    if not key:\n        return TestCustomerAiConnectionResult(failure_reason=NO_PROVIDERS_CONFIGURED)\n    verified = verify_llm_provider(provider, key, form.ai_model)\n    return TestCustomerAiConnectionResult(\n        ok=bool(verified.get(\"ok\")),\n        models=list(verified.get(\"models\") or []),\n        provider=verified.get(\"provider\") or provider,\n        failure_reason=\"\" if verified.get(\"ok\") else (verified.get(\"error\") or \"Provider authorization failed\"),\n    )\n\n\n@router.get(\n    \"/core/llm-available-models\",\n    summary=\"Admin: list models from the resolved customer or system LLM provider\",\n    tags=[\"context\"],\n)\nasync def llm_available_models(\n    session: SessionDep,\n    usr_context: UserAccessDep,\n) -> LlmAvailableModelsResult:\n    if not _ai_yaml_activated():\n        return LlmAvailableModelsResult(failure_reason=\"AI features are not enabled\")\n    denied = _require_customer_admin(usr_context)\n    if denied:\n        return LlmAvailableModelsResult(failure_reason=denied)\n    customer = _load_session_customer(session, usr_context)\n    if customer is None:\n        return LlmAvailableModelsResult(failure_reason=\"Customer not found\")\n    if _customer_llm_configured(customer):\n        provider = normalize_llm_provider(customer.ai_provider)\n        key = customer.decrypted_ai_api_key()\n        default_model = str(customer.ai_model or \"\").strip() or None\n        source = \"customer\"\n        default_is_system = False\n    elif system_llm_configured():\n        s = runtime_settings()\n        provider = normalize_llm_provider(getattr(s, \"AI_SERVICE\", \"opencode_go\"))\n        key = str(getattr(s, \"AI_API_KEY\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n        default_model = str(getattr(s, \"AI_MODEL\", \"\") or \"\").strip() or None\n        source = \"system\"\n        default_is_system = True\n    else:\n        return LlmAvailableModelsResult(failure_reason=NO_PROVIDERS_CONFIGURED)\n    verified = verify_llm_provider(provider, key, default_model)\n    if not verified.get(\"ok\"):\n        return LlmAvailableModelsResult(\n            source=source,\n            provider=provider,\n            default_model=default_model,\n            default_is_system=default_is_system,\n            failure_reason=verified.get(\"error\") or \"Provider authorization failed\",\n        )\n    return LlmAvailableModelsResult(\n        source=source,\n        provider=provider,\n        default_model=default_model,\n        default_is_system=default_is_system,\n        models=list(verified.get(\"models\") or []),\n    )\n\n\ndef bind_client_session_context_settings(settings_type: type) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Point context/vote-settings fields at the application type and rebuild schemas.\"\"\"\n    if settings_type is None:\n        return\n    annotation = settings_type | None\n    for model in (UserCustomerContextView, CustomerVoteSettingsResult):\n        model.model_fields[\"settings\"].annotation = annotation\n        model.__annotations__[\"settings\"] = annotation\n        model.model_rebuild(force=True)\n","lineStart":192,"lineEnd":537,"totalLines":537,"truncated":false}},"title":"src/pvf/api/app_context_views.py","time":{"start":1787409847905,"end":1787409848032}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_01a029edd39f7ad2b7ce96df3f9c8c6d"}},"id":"prt_029edd49c001Xa1G60JsSDj3jk","sessionID":"ses_fd613a9a0ffeanefk4okv1NNUn","messageID":"msg_029edceab00153sXlukrJq0D3A"}}

{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787410079313,"sessionID":"ses_fd613a9a0ffeanefk4okv1NNUn","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"write","callID":"call_01a029f1212e7a319df41b161f034120","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/client/composables/useVersionCheck.ts","content":"export function useVersionCheck() {\n  const config = useRuntimeConfig()\n  const { context, refreshContext } = useAuth()\n\n  const clientVersion = computed(() => {\n    const v = (config.public as Record<string, unknown>).appVersion as string | undefined\n    const s = typeof v === 'string' ? v.trim() : ''\n    return s || null\n  })\n\n  const serverVersion = computed(() => {\n    const ctx = context.value as unknown as Record<string, unknown> | null\n    const sv = ctx ? (ctx.server_version as string | null | undefined) : null\n    if (typeof sv === 'string' && sv.trim()) return sv.trim()\n    const cv = ctx ? (ctx.client_version as string | null | undefined) : null\n    if (typeof cv === 'string' && cv.trim()) return cv.trim()\n    return null\n  })\n\n  const mismatch = computed(() => {\n    const cv = clientVersion.value\n    const sv = serverVersion.value\n    if (!cv || !sv) return false\n    return cv !== sv\n  })\n\n  function storageKey(sv: string | null) {\n    return `versionBannerDismissed:${sv || 'unknown'}`\n  }\n\n  const dismissed = ref(false)\n\n  function syncDismissed() {\n    if (!import.meta.client) return\n    const sv = serverVersion.value\n    if (!sv || !mismatch.value) {\n      dismissed.value = false\n      return\n    }\n    try {\n      dismissed.value = sessionStorage.getItem(storageKey(sv)) === '1'\n    } catch {\n      dismissed.value = false\n    }\n  }\n\n  watch([serverVersion, mismatch], () => syncDismissed(), { immediate: true })\n\n  watch(mismatch, (isMismatch) => {\n    if (isMismatch && import.meta.client) {\n      // lightweight telemetry — console warn for staleness monitoring\n      // eslint-disable-next-line no-console\n      console.warn(\n        `[version] client/server mismatch client=${clientVersion.value} server=${serverVersion.value} — reload to update`,\n      )\n    }\n  })\n\n  const showBanner = computed(() => mismatch.value && !dismissed.value)\n\n  function dismiss() {\n    if (!import.meta.client) return\n    const sv = serverVersion.value\n    if (!sv) return\n    try {\n      sessionStorage.setItem(storageKey(sv), '1')\n    } catch {\n      // ignore storage errors\n    }\n    dismissed.value = true\n  }\n\n  function reload() {\n    if (!import.meta.client) return\n    window.location.reload()\n  }\n\n  let pollTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null\n\n  function startPolling() {\n    if (!import.meta.client) return\n    if (pollTimer) return\n    // re-check every 10 minutes for long-lived sessions\n    pollTimer = setInterval(() => {\n      refreshContext().catch(() => {})\n    }, 10 * 60 * 1000)\n  }\n\n  function stopPolling() {\n    if (pollTimer) {\n      clearInterval(pollTimer)\n      pollTimer = null\n    }\n  }\n\n  function onFocusReCheck() {\n    refreshContext().catch(() => {})\n  }\n\n  function onVisibilityChange() {\n    if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') {\n      refreshContext().catch(() => {})\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (import.meta.client) {\n    onMounted(() => {\n      syncDismissed()\n      startPolling()\n      window.addEventListener('focus', onFocusReCheck)\n      document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibilityChange)\n    })\n    onUnmounted(() => {\n      stopPolling()\n      window.removeEventListener('focus', onFocusReCheck)\n      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibilityChange)\n    })\n  }\n\n  return {\n    clientVersion,\n    serverVersion,\n    mismatch,\n    showBanner,\n    dismissed,\n    dismiss,\n    reload,\n    refreshContext,\n  }\n}\n"},"output":"Wrote file    (client {{ clientVersion }} → server {{ serverVersion }})\n          </span>\n        </div>\n        <div class=\"version-banner__actions\">\n          <v-btn\n            color=\"warning\"\n            variant=\"flat\"\n            size=\"small\"\n            rounded=\"lg\"\n            class=\"mr-2\"\n            @click=\"onReload\"\n          >\n            Reload\n          </v-btn>\n defineEmits<{\n  reload: []\n  dismiss: []\n}>()\n\nfunction onReload() {\n  emit('reload')\n  if (!import.meta.client) return\n  window.location.reload()\n}\n\nfunction onDismiss() {\n  emit('dismiss')\n}\n</script>\n\n<style scoped>\n.version-banner {\n  margin-bottom: 16px;\n}\n.version-banner__alert {\n  border: 1.5px solid rgba(var(--v-theme-warning), -x \"$PYTHON\" ]; then\n13:   echo \"error: $PYTHON not found; run: cd src && poetry install\" >&2\n14:   exit 1\n15: fi\n16: \n17: TARGET=\"${1:-both}\"\n18: case \"$TARGET\" in\n19:   both|sessions|session|api) ;;\n20:   *)\n21:     echo \"usage: $0 [both|sessions|api]\" >&2\n22:     exit 1\n23:     ;;\n24: esac\n25: \n26: cd \"$REPO_ROOT\"\n27: export PYTHONPATH=\"$REPO_ROOT\"\n28: \n29: \"$PYTHON\" - \"$TARGET\" <<'PY'\n30: import json\n31: import sys\n32: from pathlib import Path\n33: \n34: target = sys.argv[1]\n35: root = Path(\".\")\n36: \n37: from src.pvf.pvf_app_runner import build_external_openapi, build_sessions_openapi\n38: \n39: writes = []\n40: if target in (\"both\", \"sessions\", \"session\"):\n41:     writes.append((\"openapi_sessions.json\", build_sessions_openapi()))\n42: if target in (\"both\", \"api\"):\n43:     writes.append((\"openapi_api.json\", build_external_openapi()))\n44: \n45: for name, spec in writes:\n46:     path = root / name\n47:     path.write_text(json.dumps(spec, indent=2) + \"\\n\")\n48:     print(f\"wrote {path} ({len(spec.get('paths', {}))} paths)\")\n49: PY\n\n(End of file - total 49 lines)\n</content>","metadata":{"preview":"#!/bin/bash\n# Write repo-root OpenAPI snapshots from the live FastAPI apps.\n#   ./scripts/extract_openapi/update_openapi_json.sh           # both\n#   ./scripts/extract_openapi/update_openapi_json.sh sessions  # openapi_sessions.json\n#   ./scripts/extract_openapi/update_openapi_json.sh api       # openapi_api.json\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nREPO_ROOT=\"$(cd \"$SCRIPT_DIR/../..\" && pwd)\"\nPYTHON=\"$REPO_ROOT/src/.venv/bin/python\"\n\nif [ ! -x \"$PYTHON\" ]; then\n  echo \"error: $PYTHON not found; run: cd src && poetry install\" >&2\n  exit 1\nfi\n\nTARGET=\"${1:-both}\"\ncase \"$TARGET\" in\n  both|sessions|session|api) ;;\n  *)","truncated":false,"loaded":[],"display":{"type":"file","path":"/workspaces/powerchoice/scripts/extract_openapi/update_openapi_json.sh","text":"#!/bin/bash\n# Write repo-root OpenAPI snapshots from the live FastAPI apps.\n#   ./scripts/extract_openapi/update_openapi_json.sh           # both\n#   ./scripts/extract_openapi/update_openapi_json.sh sessions  # openapi_sessions.json\n#   ./scripts/extract_openapi/update_openapi_json.sh api       # openapi_api.json\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nREPO_ROOT=\"$(cd \"$SCRIPT_DIR/../..\" && pwd)\"\nPYTHON=\"$REPO_ROOT/src/.venv/bin/python\"\n\nif [ ! -x \"$PYTHON\" ]; then\n  echo \"error: $PYTHON not found; run: cd src && poetry install\" >&2\n  exit 1\nfi\n\nTARGET=\"${1:-both}\"\ncase \"$TARGET\" in\n  both|sessions|session|api) ;;\n  *)\n    echo \"usage: $0 [both|sessions|api]\" >&2\n    exit 1\n    ;;\nesac\n\ncd \"$REPO_ROOT\"\nexport PYTHONPATH=\"$REPO_ROOT\"\n\n\"$PYTHON\" - \"$TARGET\" <<'PY'\nimport json\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\ntarget = sys.argv[1]\nroot = Path(\".\")\n\nfrom src.pvf.pvf_app_runner import build_external_openapi, build_sessions_openapi\n\nwrites = []\nif target in (\"both\", \"sessions\", \"session\"):\n    writes.append((\"openapi_sessions.json\", build_sessions_openapi()))\nif target in (\"both\", \"api\"):\n    writes.append((\"openapi_api.json\", build_external_openapi()))\n\nfor name, spec in writes:\n    path = root / name\n    path.write_text(json.dumps(spec, indent=2) + \"\\n\")\n    print(f\"wrote {path} ({len(spec.get('paths', {}))} paths)\")\nPY","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":49,"totalLines":49,"truncated":false}},"title":"scripts/extract_openapi/update_openapi_json.sh","time":{"start":1787410285296,"end":1787410285316}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_01a029f47f0075c2816c26419f2bcaa4"}},"id":"prt_029f47f7a001zdgem6g0KBbJVl","sessionID":"ses_fd613a9a0ffeanefk4okv1NNUn","messageID":"msg_029f479af001xbZpJ2z22wYNbZ"}}

-d node_modules ]; then\n16:   echo \"Installing client dependencies...\"\n17:   npm install\n18: fi\n19: \n20: # Empty apiBase → relative same-origin requests when UI is served by FastAPI\n21: echo \"Generating static client (NUXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE=)...\"\n22: NUXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE= npm run generate\n23: \n24: if [ ! -f \"$OUTPUT_PUBLIC/index.html\" ]; then\n25:   echo \"error: generate did not produce $OUTPUT_PUBLIC/index.html\" >&2\n26:   exit 1\n27: fi\n28: \n29: # Guard: production SPA HTML must link entry CSS (missing = unstyled UI)\n30: if ! grep -q 'rel=\"stylesheet\".*entry\\..*\\.css\\|entry\\..*\\.css.*rel=\"stylesheet\"\\|/_nuxt/entry\\.' \"$OUTPUT_PUBLIC/index.html\"; then\n31:   if ! grep -q 'entry\\.[^\"]*\\.css' \"$OUTPUT_PUBLIC/index.html\"; then\n32:     echo \"error: generated index.html has no entry CSS link; check nuxt SPA build config\" >&2\n33:     echo \"  (the vite:configResolved entry→server hack must not run during generate)\" >&2\n34:     exit 1\n35:   fi\n36: fi\n37: \n38: echo \"Replacing $STATIC_CLIENT_DIR ...\"\n39: rm -rf \"$STATIC_CLIENT_DIR\"\n40: mkdir -p \"$STATIC_CLIENT_DIR\"\n41: cp -a \"$OUTPUT_PUBLIC\"/. \"$STATIC_CLIENT_DIR\"/\n42: \n43: echo \"Done. -d node_modules ]; then\n  echo \"Installing client dependencies...\"\n  npm install\nfi\n\n# Empty apiBase → relative same-origin requests when UI is served by FastAPI\necho \"Generating static client (NUXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE=)...\"\nNUXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE= npm run generate\n\nif [ ! -f \"$OUTPUT_PUBLIC/index.html\" ]; then\n  echo \"error: generate did not produce $OUTPUT_PUBLIC/index.html\" >&2\n  exit 1\nfi\n\n# Guard: production SPA HTML must link entry CSS (missing = unstyled UI)\nif ! grep -q 'rel=\"stylesheet\".*entry\\..*\\.css\\|entry\\..*\\.css.*rel=\"stylesheet\"\\|/_nuxt/entry\\.' \"$OUTPUT_PUBLIC/index.html\"; then\n  if ! grep -q 'entry\\.[^\"]*\\.css' \"$OUTPUT_PUBLIC/index.html\"; then\n    echo \"error: generated index.html has no entry CSS link; check nuxt SPA build config\" >&2\n    echo \"  (the vite:configResolved entry→server hack must not run during generate)\" >&2\n    exit 1\n  fi\nfi\n\necho \"Replacing $STATIC_CLIENT_DIR ...\"\nrm -rf \"$STATIC_CLIENT_DIR\"\nmkdir -p \"$STATIC_CLIENT_DIR\"\ncp -a \"$OUTPUT_PUBLIC\"/. \"$STATIC_CLIENT_DIR\"/\n\necho \"Done. {"type":"text","timestamp":1787410445635,"sessionID":"ses_fd613a9a0ffeanefk4okv1NNUn","part":{"id":"prt_029f6a340001aOa2vJj7Co5Bjk","messageID":"msg_029f67e0a001RnT6ChYlMdzhs0","sessionID":"ses_fd613a9a0ffeanefk4okv1NNUn","type":"text","text":"**Build Summary — client-version-in-context-to-client-14n3y0**\n\nCommit `fa5dfdfca004cf1299e8bac9066cbc658d75c55e` — `feat: expose client/server versions in context and add mismatch banner`\n\n**Backend — context payload**\n- `src/pvf/api/app_context_views.py:141` — `UserCustomerContextView` extended with `server_version: str|None` and `client_version: str|None` (both typed nullable, yellow-banner contract)\n- `src/pvf/api/app_context_views.py:95` — new `_active_version()` helper: `invocation_context.application_version` → `runtime_settings().VERSION` → `pvf_settings.PVF_VERSION`, strict string cast, graceful `None`\n- `src/pvf/api/app_context_views.py:239-253` — `get_user_customer_context()` now populates `server_version=_active, client_version=_active` on construction (covers both `/ws/core/get-user-customer-context:211` and `/auth-ws/login-and-get-context:202` via shared builder)\n- `src/config/config_settings.py:13` — `VERSION 0.7.80 → 0.7.81` (server active version source, also `GET /status` fallback via `src/pvf/pvf_app_runner.py:375`)\n\n**Client — build-time version injection**\n- `client/package.json:3` — added `\"version\": \"0.7.81\"` (client truth, kept in sync with server per `AGENTS.md`)\n- `client/nuxt.config.ts:16-20` — `runtimeConfig.public.appVersion = process.env.NUXT_PUBLIC_APP_VERSION || '0.7.81'` (baked at `nuxt build/generate`, same-origin when served from FastAPI)\n- `client/types/api.ts:59` — `UserCustomerContextView` interface adds `server_version?`/`client_version?` + `stripe_callback_prefix_sandbox?`\n\n**Frontend — comparison & banner**\n- `client/composables/useVersionCheck.ts:1` — new composable: `clientVersion = runtimeConfig.public.appVersion`, `serverVersion = context.server_version ?? client_version`, `mismatch = strict !==` with missing-guard (no false positive), `showBanner = mismatch && !dismissed`, `sessionStorage` key `versionBannerDismissed:<serverVersion>` per-session dismiss, `console.warn` telemetry, `reload() => window.location.reload()`, 10-min polling + `focus`/`visibilitychange` re-check via `refreshContext()`\n- `client/components/VersionBanner.vue:1` — new yellow banner: `v-alert type=\"warning\" color=\"warning\" variant=\"tonal\" prominent`, `role=\"alert\" aria-live=\"polite\"`, text “A new version is available — reload to update.” with `client → server` meta, `Reload` (flat warning) → `window.location.reload()` and `Dismiss` → `sessionStorage`, non-blocking flex layout above content\n- `client/layouts/default.vue:159` — banner inserted at top of `<div class=\"page-container\">` above read-only alert; `client/layouts/default.vue:184` `const versionCheck = useVersionCheck()`\n- `client/layouts/auth.vue:1` — wrapped slot in `page-container` + same banner with `useVersionCheck` (no-op when unauthenticated)\n- `client/layouts/share.vue:31,41` — same banner in share layout; `client/layouts/share.vue:44` `useVersionCheck` added\n\n**Contract & build artifacts**\n- `openapi_sessions.json` — regenerated via `src/pvf/pvf_app_runner.py:139 build_sessions_openapi()` → `openapi_sessions.json` now lists `server_version/client_version` on `UserCustomerContextView` (115 paths)\n- `static_client/` — rebuilt via `./scripts/nuxt/update_static_client.sh` (`nuxt generate` with empty `NUXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE`, hashed assets, entry CSS guard passed)\n\n**Verification**\n- `src/.venv/bin/pytest src/tests/test_context.py` — 15 passed\n- Ad-hoc `TestClient` check: `POST /ws/core/get-user-customer-context` returns `server_version: 0.7.81, client_version: 0.7.81`; `/session/docs.json` schema contains both fields\n- `./scripts/extract_openapi/update_openapi_json.sh sessions` succeeded\n- `./scripts/nuxt/update_static_client.sh` succeeded (29.5s client build, 22 routes prerendered)\n","time":{"start":1787410424640,"end":1787410445627},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"msg_01a029f6a2ed70f3a5467e27c35cc13e"}}}}
